Both models can answer complex questions about surgery and substantially outperform larger general-domain models, exceeding 84% accuracy on the SUREON benchmark while outperforming general-domain models on standard surgical perception tasks. Qualitative analysis of SureonVLM-R1 reveals explicit reasoning behavior, such as inferring operative intent from visual context.

</details>


### [75] [GenHOI: Towards Object-Consistent Hand-Object Interaction with Temporally Balanced and Spatially Selective Object Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06048)
*Xuan Huang,Mochu Xiang,Zhelun Shen,Jinbo Wu,Chenming Wu,Chen Zhao,Kaisiyuan Wang,Hang Zhou,Shanshan Liu,Haocheng Feng,Wei He,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenHOI是一个轻量级增强模块，用于预训练视频生成模型，通过时间平衡和空间选择性注入参考物体信息，提升手-物体交互视频合成的物理合理性和物体一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手-物体交互(HOI)视频合成面临两大挑战：现有HOI重演方法局限于域内场景，无法泛化到复杂野外场景；而通用视频编辑模型虽然鲁棒性更强，但在HOI特定问题上（如物体外观不一致）表现不佳。需要一种既能保持背景真实感又能增强交互保真度的解决方案。

Method: 提出GenHOI框架，包含两个核心技术：1) Head-Sliding RoPE：通过为参考标记分配头部特定的时间偏移，将参考信息均匀分布到各帧，缓解3D RoPE的时间衰减问题，改善长距离物体一致性；2) 两级空间注意力门：在空间上集中物体条件注意力于HOI区域，并自适应调整注意力强度，在保持背景真实性的同时增强交互保真度。

Result: 在未见过的野外场景上进行广泛定性和定量评估，结果表明GenHOI在HOI视频合成方面显著优于最先进的HOI重演方法和通用视频编辑方法，在物体一致性、物理合理性和视觉质量方面均有明显提升。

Conclusion: GenHOI通过时间平衡和空间选择性的参考信息注入机制，有效解决了HOI视频合成中的关键挑战，为预训练视频生成模型提供了轻量级但高效的增强方案，在复杂野外场景中实现了更好的手-物体交互合成效果。

Abstract: Hand-Object Interaction (HOI) remains a core challenge in digital human video synthesis, where models must generate physically plausible contact and preserve object identity across frames. Although recent HOI reenactment approaches have achieved progress, they are typically trained and evaluated in-domain and fail to generalize to complex, in-the-wild scenarios. In contrast, all-in-one video editing models exhibit broader robustness but still struggle with HOI-specific issues such as inconsistent object appearance. In this paper, we present GenHOI, a lightweight augmentation to pretrained video generation models that injects reference-object information in a temporally balanced and spatially selective manner. For temporal balancing, we propose Head-Sliding RoPE, which assigns head-specific temporal offsets to reference tokens, distributing their influence evenly across frames and mitigating the temporal decay of 3D RoPE to improve long-range object consistency. For spatial selectivity, we design a two-level spatial attention gate that concentrates object-conditioned attention on HOI regions and adaptively scales its strength, preserving background realism while enhancing interaction fidelity. Extensive qualitative and quantitative evaluations on unseen, in-the-wild scenes demonstrate that GenHOI significantly outperforms state-of-the-art HOI reenactment and all-in-one video editing methods. Project page: https://xuanhuang0.github.io/GenHOI/

</details>


### [76] [BEVLM: Distilling Semantic Knowledge from LLMs into Bird's-Eye View Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06576)
*Thomas Monninger,Shaoyuan Xie,Qi Alfred Chen,Sihao Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEVLM框架将空间一致的BEV表示与LLMs连接，通过BEV特征作为统一输入提升跨视图驾驶场景推理能力，并通过知识蒸馏提升端到端驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多视角多帧图像独立输入LLMs，导致计算冗余和空间一致性不足，而BEV表示虽有空间结构但缺乏语义丰富性，需要桥接两者优势。

Method: 提出BEVLM框架：1) 使用空间一致且语义蒸馏的BEV表示作为LLMs的统一输入；2) 将LLMs的语义知识蒸馏到BEV表示中；3) 通过BEV特征连接视觉处理与语言模型推理。

Result: BEVLM使LLMs在跨视图驾驶场景中的推理准确率提升46%；通过知识蒸馏到BEV表示，在安全关键场景中的闭环端到端驾驶性能提升29%。

Conclusion: BEVLM成功桥接了BEV的空间一致性与LLMs的语义理解能力，显著提升了自动驾驶系统的推理准确性和端到端驾驶性能。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into autonomous driving has attracted growing interest for their strong reasoning and semantic understanding abilities, which are essential for handling complex decision-making and long-tail scenarios. However, existing methods typically feed LLMs with tokens from multi-view and multi-frame images independently, leading to redundant computation and limited spatial consistency. This separation in visual processing hinders accurate 3D spatial reasoning and fails to maintain geometric coherence across views. On the other hand, Bird's-Eye View (BEV) representations learned from geometrically annotated tasks (e.g., object detection) provide spatial structure but lack the semantic richness of foundation vision encoders. To bridge this gap, we propose BEVLM, a framework that connects a spatially consistent and semantically distilled BEV representation with LLMs. Through extensive experiments, we show that BEVLM enables LLMs to reason more effectively in cross-view driving scenes, improving accuracy by 46%, by leveraging BEV features as unified inputs. Furthermore, by distilling semantic knowledge from LLMs into BEV representations, BEVLM significantly improves closed-loop end-to-end driving performance by 29% in safety-critical scenarios.

</details>


### [77] [Devil is in Narrow Policy: Unleashing Exploration in Driving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06049)
*Canyu Chen,Yuguang Yang,Zhewen Tan,Yizhi Wang,Ruiyi Zhan,Haiyan Liu,Xuanyao Mao,Jason Bao,Xinyue Tang,Linlin Yang,Bingchuan Sun,Yan Wang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Curious-VLA通过两阶段设计解决VLA模型中的探索-利用困境：IL阶段使用可行轨迹扩展策略生成多样化数据，RL阶段采用自适应多样性感知采样和跨度驾驶奖励来提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文发现现有VLA模型存在"窄策略限制"问题：模仿学习阶段探索不足导致后续强化学习阶段过早饱和，限制了模型的性能潜力。

Method: 提出Curious-VLA框架：1) IL阶段：采用可行轨迹扩展策略生成多个物理有效轨迹，使用逐步归一化轨迹表示适应多样化数据；2) RL阶段：引入自适应多样性感知采样优先选择高多样性样本，设计跨度驾驶奖励配合焦点风格加权来扩大奖励值跨度，提升对驾驶质量的敏感性。

Result: 在Navsim基准测试中取得SOTA结果：PDMS 90.3，EPDMS 85.4，Best-of-N PDMS达到94.8，证明了该方法能有效释放VLA模型的探索潜力。

Conclusion: Curious-VLA通过解决模仿学习中的探索限制和强化学习中的反馈多样性不足问题，成功提升了VLA模型的性能，为自动驾驶领域提供了有效的探索-利用平衡解决方案。

Abstract: We identify a fundamental Narrow Policy limitation undermining the performance of autonomous VLA models, where driving Imitation Learning (IL) tends to collapse exploration and limit the potential of subsequent Reinforcement Learning (RL) stages, which often saturate prematurely due to insufficient feedback diversity. Thereby, we propose Curious-VLA, a framework that alleviates the exploit-explore dilemma through a two-stage design. During IL, we introduce a Feasible Trajectory Expansion (FTE) strategy to generate multiple physically valid trajectories and a step-wise normalized trajectory representation to adapt this diverse data. In the RL stage, we present Adaptive Diversity-Aware Sampling (ADAS) that prioritizes high-diversity samples and introduce Spanning Driving Reward (SDR) with a focal style weighting to amplify reward's value span for improving sensitivity to driving quality. On the Navsim benchmark, Curious-VLA achieves SoTA results (PDMS 90.3, EPDMS 85.4) and a Best-of-N PDMS of 94.8, demonstrating its effectiveness in unlocking the exploratory potential of VLA models. Code: https://github.com/Mashiroln/curious_vla.git.

</details>


### [78] [FedARKS: Federated Aggregation via Robust and Discriminative Knowledge Selection and Integration for Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06122)
*Xin Xu,Binchang Ma,Zhixi Yu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedARKS框架解决FedDG-ReID中全局特征不足和均匀聚合忽略客户端差异的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦域泛化行人重识别方法依赖全局特征和简单平均聚合，难以捕获局部细节且忽略客户端能力差异

Method: 提出FedARKS框架，包含RK（鲁棒知识）和KS（知识选择）两个机制，通过鲁棒知识提取和选择性知识集成提升泛化能力

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体实验结果，但框架旨在解决现有方法的局限性

Conclusion: FedARKS框架通过鲁棒知识选择和集成机制，有望提升联邦域泛化行人重识别模型的泛化能力和隐私保护效果

Abstract: The application of federated domain generalization in person re-identification (FedDG-ReID) aims to enhance the model's generalization ability in unseen domains while protecting client data privacy. However, existing mainstream methods typically rely on global feature representations and simple averaging operations for model aggregation, leading to two limitations in domain generalization: (1) Using only global features makes it difficult to capture subtle, domain-invariant local details (such as accessories or textures); (2) Uniform parameter averaging treats all clients as equivalent, ignoring their differences in robust feature extraction capabilities, thereby diluting the contributions of high quality clients. To address these issues, we propose a novel federated learning framework, Federated Aggregation via Robust and Discriminative Knowledge Selection and Integration (FedARKS), comprising two mechanisms: RK (Robust Knowledge) and KS (Knowledge Selection).

</details>


### [79] [Cross-Resolution Distribution Matching for Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06136)
*Feiyang Chen,Hongpeng Pan,Haonan Xu,Xinyu Duan,Yang Yang,Zhefeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RMD框架，通过跨分辨率分布匹配蒸馏解决扩散蒸馏中分辨率差异导致的生成质量下降问题，实现高效多分辨率级联推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散蒸馏方法受限于去噪过程，步数减少已基本饱和；部分时间步低分辨率生成虽能加速但存在跨分辨率分布差异导致质量下降

Method: 使用对数信噪比曲线划分各分辨率时间步区间，引入logSNR映射补偿分辨率偏移；沿分辨率轨迹进行分布匹配以减少低分辨率生成分布与教师高分辨率分布间的差距；在上采样中加入预测噪声重注入机制稳定训练

Result: RMD在保持高保真生成的同时显著加速推理，在SDXL上实现33.4倍加速，在Wan2.1-14B上实现25.6倍加速

Conclusion: RMD框架有效解决了跨分辨率分布差异问题，为扩散模型的高效多分辨率级联推理提供了新方法

Abstract: Diffusion distillation is central to accelerating image and video generation, yet existing methods are fundamentally limited by the denoising process, where step reduction has largely saturated. Partial timestep low-resolution generation can further accelerate inference, but it suffers noticeable quality degradation due to cross-resolution distribution gaps. We propose Cross-Resolution Distribution Matching Distillation (RMD), a novel distillation framework that bridges cross-resolution distribution gaps for high-fidelity, few-step multi-resolution cascaded inference. Specifically, RMD divides the timestep intervals for each resolution using logarithmic signal-to-noise ratio (logSNR) curves, and introduces logSNR-based mapping to compensate for resolution-induced shifts. Distribution matching is conducted along resolution trajectories to reduce the gap between low-resolution generator distributions and the teacher's high-resolution distribution. In addition, a predicted-noise re-injection mechanism is incorporated during upsampling to stabilize training and improve synthesis quality. Quantitative and qualitative results show that RMD preserves high-fidelity generation while accelerating inference across various backbones. Notably, RMD achieves up to 33.4X speedup on SDXL and 25.6X on Wan2.1-14B, while preserving high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [80] [Spatial Colour Mixing Illusions as a Perception Stress Test for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06141)
*Nicoleta-Nina Basoc,Adrian Cosma,Emilian Radoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究了视觉语言模型在结构化颜色失真下的系统性感知弱点，发现即使人类能轻松识别，VLM的准确率也会急剧下降，且语言模型缩放无法缓解此问题。人类表现显著优于VLM，而简单的人类启发式预处理能部分恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在基准测试中表现良好，但它们存在系统性感知弱点：像素值的结构化大幅变化会导致模型做出自信但荒谬的预测，即使人类能轻松识别原始场景。研究者旨在量化这种人类与VLM之间的感知差距。

Method: 使用空间颜色混合方法，这是一种程序化的颜色失真家族，在自然图像上叠加结构化模式（包括RGB和Ostwald颜色系统）。研究者引入了八种空间颜色混合变体，评估了来自三个模型家族的九个VLM在四个数据集上的表现。同时进行了61名参与者的人类研究，比较人类与VLM在相同失真条件下的表现。最后测试了简单的人类启发式预处理步骤。

Result: 随着失真程度增加，所有模型和数据集上的准确率急剧下降；语言模型缩放无法可靠缓解这种失败；人类在相同失真条件下显著优于VLM；简单的人类启发式预处理能有效恢复几种失真类型下的部分性能。

Conclusion: VLM存在系统性感知弱点，对结构化颜色失真敏感。人类感知能力远超当前VLM。感知感知的预处理和工具使用是提高VLM鲁棒性的实用策略。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong benchmark results, yet can exhibit systematic perceptual weaknesses: structured, large changes to pixel values can cause confident yet nonsensical predictions, even when the underlying scene remains easily recognizable to humans. We study this gap using Spatial Colour Mixing, a programmatic family of colour distortions that overlays structured patterns (in both RGB and Ostwald colour systems) onto natural images. We introduce a framework of eight spatial colour mixing variants and evaluate nine VLMs across three model families on four datasets. Across models and datasets, accuracy degrades sharply with increasing distortion, and scaling the language model does not reliably mitigate the failure. In a human study with 61 participants on an animal recognition dataset, humans substantially outperform VLMs under the same distortions. Finally, we show that a simple human-inspired preprocessing step recovers a meaningful portion of performance for several distortion types, motivating perception-aware preprocessing and tool-use as practical strategies for improving VLM robustness.

</details>


### [81] [Longitudinal NSCLC Treatment Progression via Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06147)
*Massimiliano Mantegna,Elena Mulero Ayllón,Alice Natalina Caragliano,Francesco Di Feola,Claudia Tacconi,Michele Fiore,Edy Ippolito,Carlo Greco,Sara Ramella,Philippe C. Cattin,Paolo Soda,Matteo Tortora,Valerio Guarrasi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出虚拟治疗框架，将非小细胞肺癌放疗期间的肿瘤演化建模为剂量感知的多模态条件图像转换问题，用于合成反映治疗诱导解剖变化的随访CT图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测放疗期间的肿瘤演化是临床关键挑战，特别是当纵向变化同时受解剖结构和治疗影响时。现有方法难以准确模拟放疗诱导的解剖变化，需要开发能够整合剂量信息和临床变量的预测模型。

Method: 提出虚拟治疗框架，将NSCLC进展建模为剂量感知的多模态条件图像转换问题。框架基于CT扫描、基线临床变量和特定辐射剂量增量，合成反映治疗诱导解剖变化的随访CT图像。在222名III期NSCLC患者的纵向数据集（895个CT扫描）上评估，生成过程以递送剂量增量、人口统计学和肿瘤相关临床变量为条件。对基于GAN和基于扩散的模型在2D和2.5D配置上进行基准测试。

Result: 定量和定性结果表明，基于扩散的模型更一致地从多模态、剂量感知的条件中受益，比基于GAN的基线产生更稳定和解剖学上更合理的肿瘤演化轨迹。扩散模型在合成质量、稳定性和解剖合理性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 虚拟治疗框架支持将NSCLC放疗期间的肿瘤演化建模为剂量感知的图像转换问题。扩散模型在该任务中表现优于GAN，支持VT作为NSCLC数字治疗监测和自适应放疗研究工具的潜力。

Abstract: Predicting tumor evolution during radiotherapy is a clinically critical challenge, particularly when longitudinal changes are driven by both anatomy and treatment. In this work, we introduce a Virtual Treatment (VT) framework that formulates non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) progression as a dose-aware multimodal conditional image-to-image translation problem. Given a CT scan, baseline clinical variables, and a specified radiation dose increment, VT aims to synthesize plausible follow-up CT images reflecting treatment-induced anatomical changes. We evaluate the proposed framework on a longitudinal dataset of 222 stage III NSCLC patients, comprising 895 CT scans acquired during radiotherapy under irregular clinical schedules. The generative process is conditioned on delivered dose increments together with demographic and tumor-related clinical variables. Representative GAN-based and diffusion-based models are benchmarked across 2D and 2.5D configurations. Quantitative and qualitative results indicate that diffusion-based models benefit more consistently from multimodal, dose-aware conditioning and produce more stable and anatomically plausible tumor evolution trajectories than GAN-based baselines, supporting the potential of VT as a tool for in-silico treatment monitoring and adaptive radiotherapy research in NSCLC.

</details>


### [82] [FreeOcc: Training-free Panoptic Occupancy Prediction via Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06166)
*Andrew Caunes,Thierry Chateau,Vincent Fremont*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeOcc：一种无需训练的3D场景理解框架，利用预训练基础模型从多视角图像中恢复语义和几何信息，实现语义和全景占用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于相机的3D场景理解方法通常需要昂贵的密集3D监督或针对特定领域数据进行训练，限制了在未见环境中的部署。需要一种无需训练的方法来解决这些限制。

Method: 1) 使用可提示的基础分割模型提取每视角全景先验，通过提示到分类规则处理；2) 使用重建基础模型恢复度量3D点；3) 通过深度和置信度感知过滤将可靠标签提升到3D空间；4) 随时间融合并体素化，使用确定性细化堆栈；5) 通过拟合和合并鲁棒的当前视角3D框候选恢复实例，实现无需学习3D模型的实例感知占用。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上，FreeOcc无需训练达到16.9 mIoU和16.5 RayIoU，与最先进的弱监督方法相当。作为伪标签生成管道训练下游模型时，达到21.1 RayIoU，超越先前最先进的弱监督基线。在全景占用预测方面，无需训练和弱监督设置分别达到3.1 RayPQ和3.9 RayPQ，创下新基线。

Conclusion: FreeOcc证明了基于基础模型的感知是实现无需训练3D场景理解的实用途径，为语义和全景占用预测提供了有效的训练免费解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic and panoptic occupancy prediction for road scene analysis provides a dense 3D representation of the ego vehicle's surroundings. Current camera-only approaches typically rely on costly dense 3D supervision or require training models on data from the target domain, limiting deployment in unseen environments. We propose FreeOcc, a training-free pipeline that leverages pretrained foundation models to recover both semantics and geometry from multi-view images. FreeOcc extracts per-view panoptic priors with a promptable foundation segmentation model and prompt-to-taxonomy rules, and reconstructs metric 3D points with a reconstruction foundation model. Depth- and confidence- aware filtering lifts reliable labels into 3D, which are fused over time and voxelized with a deterministic refinement stack. For panoptic occupancy, instances are recovered by fitting and merging robust current-view 3D box candidates, enabling instance-aware occupancy without any learned 3D model. On Occ3D-nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves 16.9 mIoU and 16.5 RayIoU train-free, on par with state-of-the-art weakly supervised methods. When employed as a pseudo-label generation pipeline for training downstream models, it achieves 21.1 RayIoU, surpassing the previous state-of-the-art weakly supervised baseline. Furthermore, FreeOcc sets new baselines for both train-free and weakly supervised panoptic occupancy prediction, achieving 3.1 RayPQ and 3.9 RayPQ, respectively. These results highlight foundation-model-driven perception as a practical route to training-free 3D scene understanding.

</details>


### [83] [A Semi-Supervised Framework for Breast Ultrasound Segmentation with Training-Free Pseudo-Label Generation and Label Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06167)
*Ruili Li,Jiayi Ding,Ruiyu Li,Yilun Jin,Shiwen Ge,Yuwen Zeng,Xiaoyong Zhang,Eichi Takaya,Jan Vrba,Noriyasu Homma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于乳腺超声图像分割的半监督框架，通过免训练的伪标签生成和标签精炼，在仅有2.5%标注数据下达到全监督模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习在标注极度有限时伪标签不稳定，导致监督不准确和性能下降。现有视觉语言模型在乳腺超声图像上效果有限，因为领域特定提示难以迁移。

Method: 1) 基于简单外观描述实现跨域结构迁移，让视觉语言模型生成结构一致的伪标签；2) 用伪标签预热静态教师模型捕获乳腺病灶的全局结构先验；3) 结合指数移动平均教师，引入不确定性熵加权融合和自适应不确定性引导的反向对比学习来改进边界判别

Result: 在四个乳腺超声数据集上，仅使用2.5%标注数据即达到全监督模型可比性能，显著优于现有半监督方法

Conclusion: 该范式易于扩展：对于其他成像模态或疾病，仅需全局外观描述即可获得可靠的伪监督，实现有限标注下的可扩展半监督医学图像分割

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for breast ultrasound (BUS) image segmentation, but it often suffers from unstable pseudo labels under extremely limited annotations, leading to inaccurate supervision and degraded performance. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) provide a new opportunity for pseudo-label generation, yet their effectiveness on BUS images remains limited because domain-specific prompts are difficult to transfer.
  To address this issue, we propose a semi-supervised framework with training-free pseudo-label generation and label refinement. By leveraging simple appearance-based descriptions (e.g., dark oval), our method enables cross-domain structural transfer between natural and medical images, allowing VLMs to generate structurally consistent pseudo labels. These pseudo labels are used to warm up a static teacher that captures global structural priors of breast lesions. Combined with an exponential moving average teacher, we further introduce uncertainty entropy weighted fusion and adaptive uncertainty-guided reverse contrastive learning to improve boundary discrimination.
  Experiments on four BUS datasets demonstrate that our method achieves performance comparable to fully supervised models even with only 2.5% labeled data, significantly outperforming existing SSL approaches. Moreover, the proposed paradigm is readily extensible: for other imaging modalities or diseases, only a global appearance description is required to obtain reliable pseudo supervision, enabling scalable semi-supervised medical image segmentation under limited annotations.

</details>


### [84] [JOPP-3D: Joint Open Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation on Point Clouds and Panoramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06168)
*Sandeep Inuganti,Hideaki Kanayama,Kanta Shimizu,Mahdi Chamseddine,Soichiro Yokota,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JOPP-3D是一个开放词汇语义分割框架，通过联合利用全景图像和点云数据，实现语言驱动的场景理解，在开放和封闭词汇的2D和3D语义分割上相比SOTA有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨视觉模态（3D点云和全景图像）语义分割的挑战，主要由于标注数据稀缺和固定标签模型适应性有限。需要开发能够通过自然语言查询生成语义掩码的开放词汇框架。

Method: 将RGB-D全景图像转换为对应的切向透视图像和3D点云，利用这些模态提取和对齐基础视觉语言特征。通过自然语言查询在两种输入模态上生成语义掩码。

Result: 在Stanford-2D-3D-s和ToF-360数据集上的实验表明，JOPP-3D能够在全景和3D域上产生连贯且语义有意义的分割。相比SOTA在开放和封闭词汇的2D和3D语义分割上取得显著改进。

Conclusion: JOPP-3D框架通过联合处理全景和点云数据，实现了有效的开放词汇语义分割，为语言驱动的跨模态场景理解提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation across visual modalities such as 3D point clouds and panoramic images remains a challenging task, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated data and the limited adaptability of fixed-label models. In this paper, we present JOPP-3D, an open-vocabulary semantic segmentation framework that jointly leverages panoramic and point cloud data to enable language-driven scene understanding. We convert RGB-D panoramic images into their corresponding tangential perspective images and 3D point clouds, then use these modalities to extract and align foundational vision-language features. This allows natural language querying to generate semantic masks on both input modalities. Experimental evaluation on the Stanford-2D-3D-s and ToF-360 datasets demonstrates the capability of JOPP-3D to produce coherent and semantically meaningful segmentations across panoramic and 3D domains. Our proposed method achieves a significant improvement compared to the SOTA in open and closed vocabulary 2D and 3D semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [85] [Optimizing 3D Diffusion Models for Medical Imaging via Multi-Scale Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06173)
*Yueying Tian,Xudong Han,Meng Zhou,Rodrigo Aviles-Espinosa,Rupert Young,Philip Birch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种使用强化学习（RL）和多尺度反馈来增强3D扩散模型的方法，通过结合2D切片评估和3D体积分析来优化局部纹理细节和全局结构一致性，在医学图像生成任务中取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D医学图像生成中表现出强大能力，但标准训练目标与临床相关性之间存在差距。需要开发一种方法，使生成的图像不仅统计上合理，而且在临床任务中具有实际效用。

Method: 首先在MRI体积数据上预训练3D扩散模型建立生成先验，然后使用近端策略优化（PPO）进行微调。微调过程采用新颖的奖励系统，整合了2D切片级评估和3D体积分析，使模型能同时优化局部纹理细节和全局结构一致性。

Result: 在BraTS 2019和OASIS-1数据集上的验证表明，RL反馈有效引导生成过程向更高质量分布发展。定量分析显示Fréchet Inception Distance（FID）显著改善，且合成数据在肿瘤和疾病分类任务中比未优化基线表现出更强的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习与多尺度反馈的结合，成功提升了3D扩散模型在医学图像生成中的质量，使生成的图像不仅统计上合理，而且在临床下游任务中具有实际应用价值，为医学图像合成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for 3D medical image generation, yet bridging the gap between standard training objectives and clinical relevance remains a challenge. This paper presents a method to enhance 3D diffusion models using Reinforcement Learning (RL) with multi-scale feedback. We first pretrain a 3D diffusion model on MRI volumes to establish a robust generative prior. Subsequently, we fine-tune the model using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), guided by a novel reward system that integrates both 2D slice-wise assessments and 3D volumetric analysis. This combination allows the model to simultaneously optimize for local texture details and global structural coherence. We validate our framework on the BraTS 2019 and OASIS-1 datasets. Our results indicate that incorporating RL feedback effectively steers the generation process toward higher quality distributions. Quantitative analysis reveals significant improvements in Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and, crucially, the synthetic data demonstrates enhanced utility in downstream tumor and disease classification tasks compared to non-optimized baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Making Training-Free Diffusion Segmentors Scale with the Generative Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06178)
*Benyuan Meng,Qianqian Xu,Zitai Wang,Xiaochun Cao,Longtao Huang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术（自动聚合和逐像素重缩放）来解决训练免费扩散分割器中的两个关键问题：跨注意力图与全局表示之间的差异，以及文本标记间的分数不平衡问题，从而让训练免费分割更好地利用生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费扩散分割方法通常依赖扩散模型的跨注意力图来捕捉像素与文本标记之间的语义关系。理论上，更强的生成能力应该带来更好的分割效果，但现有方法未能按比例扩展。研究发现存在两个关键问题：跨注意力图在多头和多层计算中存在差异，且全局图不能直接转化为准确的分割语义相关性。

Method: 提出两种核心技术：1) 自动聚合：解决多头多层跨注意力图与全局表示之间的差异；2) 逐像素重缩放：解决不同文本标记间的分数不平衡问题，使训练免费分割能更好地利用生成模型的生成能力。

Result: 在标准语义分割基准上评估了该方法，并进一步将其集成到生成技术中，展示了改进的性能和广泛的适用性。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 通过自动聚合和逐像素重缩放技术，成功解决了训练免费扩散分割器中的关键问题，使分割性能能够更好地随生成模型能力的增强而提升，为训练免费分割方法提供了有效的改进方案。

Abstract: As powerful generative models, text-to-image diffusion models have recently been explored for discriminative tasks. A line of research focuses on adapting a pre-trained diffusion model to semantic segmentation without any further training, leading to what training-free diffusion segmentors. These methods typically rely on cross-attention maps from the model's attention layers, which are assumed to capture semantic relationships between image pixels and text tokens. Ideally, such approaches should benefit from more powerful diffusion models, i.e., stronger generative capability should lead to better segmentation. However, we observe that existing methods often fail to scale accordingly. To understand this issue, we identify two underlying gaps: (i) cross-attention is computed across multiple heads and layers, but there exists a discrepancy between these individual attention maps and a unified global representation. (ii) Even when a global map is available, it does not directly translate to accurate semantic correlation for segmentation, due to score imbalances among different text tokens. To bridge these gaps, we propose two techniques: auto aggregation and per-pixel rescaling, which together enable training-free segmentation to better leverage generative capability. We evaluate our approach on standard semantic segmentation benchmarks and further integrate it into a generative technique, demonstrating both improved performance broad applicability. Codes are at https://github.com/Darkbblue/goca.

</details>


### [87] [Point-Supervised Skeleton-Based Human Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06201)
*Hongsong Wang,Yiqin Shen,Pengbo Yan,Jie Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于骨骼时序动作分割的点监督框架，仅需每个动作片段标注一帧，通过多模态骨骼数据、原型相似度方法和伪标签集成实现高效分割


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决全监督方法需要昂贵帧级标注和对模糊动作边界敏感的问题，降低标注成本同时保持性能

Method: 使用预训练统一模型编码关节、骨骼和运动多模态特征；提出原型相似度方法，结合能量函数和约束K-Medoids聚类生成伪标签；采用多模态伪标签集成指导模型训练

Result: 在PKU-MMD(X-Sub/X-View)、MCFS-22和MCFS-130数据集上建立新基准，性能具有竞争力，甚至超越部分全监督方法，显著减少标注工作量

Conclusion: 点监督框架有效降低骨骼动作分割的标注成本，多模态特征和伪标签集成策略是关键，为弱监督动作分割提供新思路

Abstract: Skeleton-based temporal action segmentation is a fundamental yet challenging task, playing a crucial role in enabling intelligent systems to perceive and respond to human activities. While fully-supervised methods achieve satisfactory performance, they require costly frame-level annotations and are sensitive to ambiguous action boundaries. To address these issues, we introduce a point-supervised framework for skeleton-based action segmentation, where only a single frame per action segment is labeled. We leverage multimodal skeleton data, including joint, bone, and motion information, encoded via a pretrained unified model to extract rich feature representations. To generate reliable pseudo-labels, we propose a novel prototype similarity method and integrate it with two existing methods: energy function and constrained K-Medoids clustering. Multimodal pseudo-label integration is proposed to enhance the reliability of the pseudo-label and guide the model training. We establish new benchmarks on PKU-MMD (X-Sub and X-View), MCFS-22, and MCFS-130, and implement baselines for point-supervised skeleton-based human action segmentation. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves competitive performance, even surpassing some fully-supervised methods while significantly reducing annotation effort.

</details>


### [88] [VG3S: Visual Geometry Grounded Gaussian Splatting for Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06210)
*Xiaoyang Yan,Muleilan Pei,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VG3S框架通过将视觉基础模型的几何先验注入3D高斯泼溅，显著提升了自动驾驶场景的3D语义占据预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于3D高斯泼溅的占据预测方法依赖于准确的几何线索，但在纯视觉中心范式中这些线索往往不足。需要将视觉基础模型强大的几何基础能力注入占据预测任务中。

Method: 提出VG3S框架，通过即插即用的分层几何特征适配器，将冻结的视觉基础模型的3D几何先验转化为适合占据预测的特征表示。该适配器通过特征聚合、任务特定对齐和多尺度重构来转换通用VFM令牌。

Result: 在nuScenes占据基准测试中，VG3S相比基线在IoU上提升了12.6%，在mIoU上提升了7.5%。框架能够无缝泛化到不同的视觉基础模型，一致地提升占据预测准确性。

Conclusion: VG3S证明了将来自强大预训练几何基础视觉基础模型的先验集成到占据预测中的巨大价值，为自动驾驶场景理解提供了更准确的3D几何建模能力。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction has become a crucial perception task for comprehensive scene understanding in autonomous driving. While recent advances have explored 3D Gaussian splatting for occupancy modeling to substantially reduce computational overhead, the generation of high-quality 3D Gaussians relies heavily on accurate geometric cues, which are often insufficient in purely vision-centric paradigms. To bridge this gap, we advocate for injecting the strong geometric grounding capability from Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) into occupancy prediction. In this regard, we introduce Visual Geometry Grounded Gaussian Splatting (VG3S), a novel framework that empowers Gaussian-based occupancy prediction with cross-view 3D geometric grounding. Specifically, to fully exploit the rich 3D geometric priors from a frozen VFM, we propose a plug-and-play hierarchical geometric feature adapter, which can effectively transform generic VFM tokens via feature aggregation, task-specific alignment, and multi-scale restructuring. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes occupancy benchmark demonstrate that VG3S achieves remarkable improvements of 12.6% in IoU and 7.5% in mIoU over the baseline. Furthermore, we show that VG3S generalizes seamlessly across diverse VFMs, consistently enhancing occupancy prediction accuracy and firmly underscoring the immense value of integrating priors derived from powerful, pre-trained geometry-grounded VFMs.

</details>


### [89] [EntON: Eigenentropy-Optimized Neighborhood Densification in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06216)
*Miriam Jäger,Boris Jutzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EntON是一种用于3D高斯泼溅的Eigenentropy优化邻域致密化策略，通过交替使用基于梯度的致密化和基于Eigenentropy的致密化，在保持渲染质量的同时提升几何精度并减少高斯数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS产生的高斯中心与底层物体几何对齐不佳，而专注于表面的重建方法常常牺牲光度精度。需要一种既能保持渲染质量又能提升几何精度的策略。

Method: 提出Eigenentropy优化的邻域致密化策略EntON：1) 从每个高斯中心k近邻的协方差矩阵特征值计算3D形状特征Eigenentropy，量化局部结构有序度；2) 在交替优化框架中结合标准梯度致密化和Eigenentropy感知致密化，优先在低Eigenentropy（有序、平坦）区域致密化以捕捉表面细节，在高Eigenentropy（无序、球形）区域修剪。

Result: 在DTU和TUM2TWIN数据集上的实验表明：几何精度提升达33%，渲染质量提升达7%，高斯数量减少达50%，训练时间减少达23%。

Conclusion: EntON通过避免不必要的场景扩展，在几何精度、渲染质量和效率之间实现了有利的平衡，为3D高斯泼溅提供了一种几何感知的致密化策略。

Abstract: We present a novel Eigenentropy-optimized neighboorhood densification strategy EntON in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for geometrically accurate and high-quality rendered 3D reconstruction. While standard 3DGS produces Gaussians whose centers and surfaces are poorly aligned with the underlying object geometry, surface-focused reconstruction methods frequently sacrifice photometric accuracy. In contrast to the conventional densification strategy, which relies on the magnitude of the view-space position gradient, our approach introduces a geometry-aware strategy to guide adaptive splitting and pruning. Specifically, we compute the 3D shape feature Eigenentropy from the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix in the k-nearest neighborhood of each Gaussian center, which quantifies the local structural order. These Eigenentropy values are integrated into an alternating optimization framework: During the optimization process, the algorithm alternates between (i) standard gradient-based densification, which refines regions via view-space gradients, and (ii) Eigenentropy-aware densification, which preferentially densifies Gaussians in low-Eigenentropy (ordered, flat) neighborhoods to better capture fine geometric details on the object surface, and prunes those in high-Eigenentropy (disordered, spherical) regions. We provide quantitative and qualitative evaluations on two benchmark datasets: small-scale DTU dataset and large-scale TUM2TWIN dataset, covering man-made objects and urban scenes. Experiments demonstrate that our Eigenentropy-aware alternating densification strategy improves geometric accuracy by up to 33% and rendering quality by up to 7%, while reducing the number of Gaussians by up to 50% and training time by up to 23%. Overall, EnTON achieves a favorable balance between geometric accuracy, rendering quality and efficiency by avoiding unnecessary scene expansion.

</details>


### [90] [Word-Anchored Temporal Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06220)
*Tianyi Wang,Xi Shao,Harry Cheng,Yinglong Wang,Mohan Kankanhalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAFL提出了一种基于词级二分类的时间伪造定位新范式，通过将伪造单元对齐到语音的自然语言边界，结合特征重对齐模块和不对称损失函数，显著提升了定位性能并降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间伪造定位方法存在特征粒度不对齐和计算成本高的问题。传统方法依赖时间边界回归或连续帧级异常检测，但这些方法在特征表示和计算效率方面存在局限性。

Method: 1) 将时间伪造定位任务从时间回归和连续定位转换为离散词级二分类；2) 识别最小有意义的伪造单元——词标记，并将数据预处理与语音的自然语言边界对齐；3) 引入法证特征重对齐模块，将预训练语义空间表示映射到判别性法证流形；4) 设计基于伪影的非对称损失函数，动态抑制真实样本梯度并优先处理细微法证伪影。

Result: WAFL在数据集内和跨数据集设置下，定位性能显著优于现有最先进方法，同时需要更少的可学习参数，并保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过将时间伪造定位重新定义为词级二分类任务，并采用特征重对齐和非对称损失函数，WAFL解决了传统方法的特征粒度不对齐和计算成本问题，为时间伪造检测提供了高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Current temporal forgery localization (TFL) approaches typically rely on temporal boundary regression or continuous frame-level anomaly detection paradigms to derive candidate forgery proposals. However, they suffer not only from feature granularity misalignment but also from costly computation. To address these issues, we propose word-anchored temporal forgery localization (WAFL), a novel paradigm that shifts the TFL task from temporal regression and continuous localization to discrete word-level binary classification. Specifically, we first analyze the essence of temporal forgeries and identify the minimum meaningful forgery units, word tokens, and then align data preprocessing with the natural linguistic boundaries of speech. To adapt powerful pre-trained foundation backbones for feature extraction, we introduce the forensic feature realignment (FFR) module, mapping representations from the pre-trained semantic space to a discriminative forensic manifold. This allows subsequent lightweight linear classifiers to efficiently perform binary classification and accomplish the TFL task. Furthermore, to overcome the extreme class imbalance inherent to forgery detection, we design the artifact-centric asymmetric (ACA) loss, which breaks the standard precision-recall trade-off by dynamically suppressing overwhelming authentic gradients while asymmetrically prioritizing subtle forensic artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WAFL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in localization performance under both in- and cross-dataset settings, while requiring substantially fewer learnable parameters and operating at high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [91] [Hierarchical Collaborative Fusion for 3D Instance-aware Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06250)
*Keshen Zhou,Runnan Chen,Mingming Gong,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HCF-RES：一种用于3D指称表达分割的多模态框架，通过层次化视觉语义分解和渐进式多级融合，在稀疏点云基础上引入2D视觉语义，显著提升对细粒度描述的定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D指称表达分割方法仅依赖稀疏点云，缺乏丰富的视觉语义信息，难以处理匹配多个或零目标的复杂自然语言描述，需要融合多模态信息来提升细粒度定位能力。

Method: 提出HCF-RES框架：1）层次化视觉语义分解：利用SAM实例掩码指导CLIP编码，获取像素级和实例级双重粒度特征，在2D到3D投影时保持物体边界；2）渐进式多级融合：通过模态内协作、2D语义与3D几何特征的跨模态自适应加权，以及语言引导的细化来集成多模态表示。

Result: 在ScanRefer和Multi3DRefer两个基准数据集上均取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: HCF-RES通过有效融合2D视觉语义和3D几何信息，显著提升了3D指称表达分割的性能，特别是在处理复杂自然语言描述时表现出色。

Abstract: Generalised 3D Referring Expression Segmentation (3D-GRES) localizes objects in 3D scenes based on natural language, even when descriptions match multiple or zero targets. Existing methods rely solely on sparse point clouds, lacking rich visual semantics for fine-grained descriptions. We propose HCF-RES, a multi-modal framework with two key innovations. First, Hierarchical Visual Semantic Decomposition leverages SAM instance masks to guide CLIP encoding at dual granularities -- pixel-level and instance-level features -- preserving object boundaries during 2D-to-3D projection. Second, Progressive Multi-level Fusion integrates representations through intra-modal collaboration, cross-modal adaptive weighting between 2D semantic and 3D geometric features, and language-guided refinement. HCF-RES achieves state-of-the-art results on both ScanRefer and Multi3DRefer.

</details>


### [92] [NOVA: Next-step Open-Vocabulary Autoregression for 3D Multi-Object Tracking in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06254)
*Kai Luo,Xu Wang,Rui Fan,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA提出了一种创新的开放词汇自回归3D多目标跟踪范式，通过将轨迹建模为结构化时空语义序列，利用LLM的自回归能力实现跨未知目标的泛化跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D多目标跟踪方法受限于闭集假设和"语义盲"启发式规则，无法有效处理开放世界中的未知目标。需要一种能够泛化到未知目标、同时利用语义信息的方法。

Method: 将3D轨迹重新定义为结构化时空语义序列，利用大语言模型的自回归能力，将跟踪任务转化为基于原则的下一步序列补全过程。通过语言空间的层次结构解决细粒度语义歧义，利用高层常识推理保持复杂长程序列的身份一致性。

Result: 在nuScenes、V2X-Seq-SPD和KITTI数据集上表现出优越性能。在nuScenes数据集上，NOVA在Novel类别上达到22.41%的AMOTA，相比基线有20.21%的绝对提升，仅使用0.5B参数的自回归模型。

Conclusion: NOVA通过将3D跟踪从传统的碎片化距离匹配转向生成式时空语义建模，成功解决了开放世界感知中的泛化问题，为3D多目标跟踪提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Generalizing across unknown targets is critical for open-world perception, yet existing 3D Multi-Object Tracking (3D MOT) pipelines remain limited by closed-set assumptions and ``semantic-blind'' heuristics. To address this, we propose Next-step Open-Vocabulary Autoregression (NOVA), an innovative paradigm that shifts 3D tracking from traditional fragmented distance-based matching toward generative spatio-temporal semantic modeling. NOVA reformulates 3D trajectories as structured spatio-temporal semantic sequences, enabling the simultaneous encoding of physical motion continuity and deep linguistic priors. By leveraging the autoregressive capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), we transform the tracking task into a principled process of next-step sequence completion. This mechanism allows the model to explicitly utilize the hierarchical structure of language space to resolve fine-grained semantic ambiguities and maintain identity consistency across complex long-range sequences through high-level commonsense reasoning. Extensive experiments on nuScenes, V2X-Seq-SPD, and KITTI demonstrate the superior performance of NOVA. Notably, on the nuScenes dataset, NOVA achieves an AMOTA of 22.41% for Novel categories, yielding a significant 20.21% absolute improvement over the baseline. These gains are realized through a compact 0.5B autoregressive model. Code will be available at https://github.com/xifen523/NOVA.

</details>


### [93] [Spectral and Trajectory Regularization for Diffusion Transformer Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06275)
*Jingkai Wang,Yixin Tang,Jue Gong,Jiatong Li,Shu Li,Libo Liu,Jianliang Lan,Yutong Liu,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StrSR框架，通过谱和轨迹正则化解决扩散变换器在真实图像超分辨率中的一步蒸馏问题，消除网格状伪影并提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变换器在真实图像超分辨率中潜力巨大，但其迭代采样计算成本高，需要一步蒸馏。现有的一步蒸馏方法在DiT上存在轨迹不匹配和严重网格状周期性伪影的问题

Method: 提出StrSR框架，包含非对称判别蒸馏架构来弥合轨迹差距，以及频率分布匹配策略来抑制DiT特有的高频谱泄漏引起的周期性伪影

Result: 大量实验表明，StrSR在真实图像超分辨率中实现了最先进的性能，在定量指标和视觉感知方面均表现出色

Conclusion: StrSR通过谱和轨迹正则化有效解决了DiT在真实图像超分辨率中的一步蒸馏问题，消除了网格状伪影，提升了性能

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) architectures show great potential for real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR). However, their computationally expensive iterative sampling necessitates one-step distillation. Existing one-step distillation methods struggle with Real-ISR on DiT. They suffer from fundamental trajectory mismatch and generate severe grid-like periodic artifacts. To tackle these challenges, we propose StrSR, a novel one-step adversarial distillation framework featuring spectral and trajectory regularization. Specifically, we propose an asymmetric discriminative distillation architecture to bridge the trajectory gap. Additionally, we design a frequency distribution matching strategy to effectively suppress DiT-specific periodic artifacts caused by high-frequency spectral leakage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StrSR achieves state-of-the-art performance in Real-ISR, across both quantitative metrics and visual perception. The code and models will be released at https://github.com/jkwang28/StrSR .

</details>


### [94] [Can we Trust Unreliable Voxels? Exploring 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06279)
*Wenxin Li,Kunyu Peng,Di Wen,Junwei Zheng,Jiale Wei,Mengfei Duan,Yuheng Zhang,Rui Fan,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对3D语义占据预测中真实世界体素标注存在的结构伪影和动态拖尾效应问题，提出了首个专门针对占据不对称和动态拖尾噪声的基准测试OccNL，并开发了基于双源部分标签推理的噪声鲁棒框架DPR-Occ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D语义占据预测是机器人感知的基础，但真实世界的体素标注存在结构伪影和动态拖尾效应等固有噪声。这引发了一个关键但未被充分探索的问题：自主系统能否安全依赖这种不可靠的占据监督？现有2D标签噪声学习策略在稀疏3D体素空间中会灾难性崩溃，暴露了现有范式的关键脆弱性。

Method: 提出了DPR-Occ框架，这是一种基于双源部分标签推理的原则性标签噪声鲁棒框架。通过协同时间模型记忆和表示级结构相似性，动态扩展和修剪候选标签集，以保留真实语义同时抑制噪声传播。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI上的大量实验表明，DPR-Occ在极端噪声下防止了几何和语义崩溃。即使在90%标签噪声下，该方法相比现有标签噪声学习基线（适应3D占据预测任务）实现了显著性能提升（最高2.57% mIoU和13.91% IoU）。

Conclusion: 通过桥接标签噪声学习和3D感知，OccNL和DPR-Occ为动态环境中安全关键的机器人感知提供了可靠基础。该基准测试和源代码将在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction is a cornerstone of robotic perception, yet real-world voxel annotations are inherently corrupted by structural artifacts and dynamic trailing effects. This raises a critical but underexplored question: can autonomous systems safely rely on such unreliable occupancy supervision? To systematically investigate this issue, we establish OccNL, the first benchmark dedicated to 3D occupancy under occupancy-asymmetric and dynamic trailing noise. Our analysis reveals a fundamental domain gap: state-of-the-art 2D label noise learning strategies collapse catastrophically in sparse 3D voxel spaces, exposing a critical vulnerability in existing paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose DPR-Occ, a principled label noise-robust framework that constructs reliable supervision through dual-source partial label reasoning. By synergizing temporal model memory with representation-level structural affinity, DPR-Occ dynamically expands and prunes candidate label sets to preserve true semantics while suppressing noise propagation. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI demonstrate that DPR-Occ prevents geometric and semantic collapse under extreme corruption. Notably, even at 90% label noise, our method achieves significant performance gains (up to 2.57% mIoU and 13.91% IoU) over existing label noise learning baselines adapted to the 3D occupancy prediction task. By bridging label noise learning and 3D perception, OccNL and DPR-Occ provide a reliable foundation for safety-critical robotic perception in dynamic environments. The benchmark and source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/mylwx/OccNL.

</details>


### [95] [Attribute Distribution Modeling and Semantic-Visual Alignment for Generative Zero-shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06281)
*Haojie Pu,Zhuoming Li,Yongbiao Gao,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADiVA方法通过联合建模属性分布和显式语义-视觉对齐，解决生成式零样本学习中的类-实例差距和语义-视觉域差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式零样本学习通过合成未见类特征来转移知识，但面临两个内在挑战：1) 类级属性无法捕捉实例特定的视觉外观（类-实例差距）；2) 语义与视觉特征分布不匹配（语义-视觉域差距）。

Method: 提出ADiVA方法，包含两个模块：1) 属性分布建模(ADM)模块，学习每个类的可转移属性分布，为未见类采样实例级属性；2) 视觉引导对齐(VGA)模块，精炼语义表示以更好反映视觉结构。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的基准数据集上，ADiVA显著优于最先进方法（在AWA2和SUN上分别获得4.7%和6.1%的提升），且可作为插件增强现有生成式ZSL方法。

Conclusion: ADiVA通过联合建模属性分布和执行显式语义-视觉对齐，有效解决了生成式零样本学习中的类-实例差距和语义-视觉域差距问题，提升了性能并具有良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Generative zero-shot learning (ZSL) synthesizes features for unseen classes, leveraging semantic conditions to transfer knowledge from seen classes. However, it also introduces two intrinsic challenges: (1) class-level attributes fails to capture instance-specific visual appearances due to substantial intra-class variability, thus causing the class-instance gap; (2) the substantial mismatch between semantic and visual feature distributions, manifested in inter-class correlations, gives rise to the semantic-visual domain gap. To address these challenges, we propose an Attribute Distribution Modeling and Semantic-Visual Alignment (ADiVA) approach, jointly modeling attribute distributions and performing explicit semantic-visual alignment. Specifically, our ADiVA consists of two modules: an Attribute Distribution Modeling (ADM) module that learns a transferable attribute distribution for each class and samples instance-level attributes for unseen classes, and a Visual-Guided Alignment (VGA) module that refines semantic representations to better reflect visual structures. Experiments on three widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate that ADiVA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods (e.g., achieving gains of 4.7% and 6.1% on AWA2 and SUN, respectively). Moreover, our approach can serve as a plugin to enhance existing generative ZSL methods.

</details>


### [96] [FlowMotion: Training-Free Flow Guidance for Video Motion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06289)
*Zhen Wang,Youcan Xu,Jun Xiao,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowMotion：一种基于预训练流模型的免训练视频运动迁移框架，通过提取潜在预测中的运动表示实现高效灵活的运动对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练方法依赖T2V模型中间输出构建运动指导，计算开销大且灵活性有限。本文旨在开发更高效灵活的运动迁移方法，直接利用流基T2V模型的预测输出。

Method: 提出FlowMotion框架：1）流指导：从早期潜在预测中提取运动表示，对齐源视频与生成视频的运动模式；2）速度正则化：稳定优化并确保平滑运动演化。完全基于模型预测操作，无需额外训练。

Result: 相比现有方法，FlowMotion在时间和资源效率上表现优异，同时保持有竞争力的性能。实现了高效且灵活的视频运动迁移。

Conclusion: FlowMotion通过直接利用流基T2V模型的预测输出，提供了一种高效、灵活且免训练的视频运动迁移解决方案，在计算效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Video motion transfer aims to generate a target video that inherits motion patterns from a source video while rendering new scenes. Existing training-free approaches focus on constructing motion guidance based on the intermediate outputs of pre-trained T2V models, which results in heavy computational overhead and limited flexibility. In this paper, we present FlowMotion, a novel training-free framework that enables efficient and flexible motion transfer by directly leveraging the predicted outputs of flow-based T2V models. Our key insight is that early latent predictions inherently encode rich temporal information. Motivated by this, we propose flow guidance, which extracts motion representations based on latent predictions to align motion patterns between source and generated videos. We further introduce a velocity regularization strategy to stabilize optimization and ensure smooth motion evolution. By operating purely on model predictions, FlowMotion achieves superior time and resource efficiency as well as competitive performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [97] [3D CBCT Artefact Removal Using Perpendicular Score-Based Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06300)
*Susanne Schaub,Florentin Bieder,Matheus L. Oliveira,Yulan Wang,Dorothea Dagassan-Berndt,Michael M. Bornstein,Philippe C. Cattin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于垂直分数扩散模型的3D牙科种植体修复方法，通过结合两个不同平面的2D扩散模型来建模投影序列的3D分布，有效减少CBCT图像中的伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT在牙科3D成像中广泛应用，但高密度物体（如牙科种植体）会产生严重伪影，影响图像质量和诊断准确性。现有基于扩散模型的种植体修复方法仅处理独立的2D投影，忽略了投影间的相关性，导致重建图像不一致。

Method: 提出3D牙科种植体修复方法，基于垂直分数扩散模型。训练两个在不同平面上的2D分数扩散模型，并在采样方案中结合这两个模型来建模投影序列的3D分布。该方法在投影域中操作，能够保持投影间的一致性。

Result: 该方法能有效生成高质量、伪影减少的3D CBCT图像，证明其在改善临床成像方面的潜力。通过建模投影序列的3D分布，解决了现有2D方法导致的图像不一致问题。

Conclusion: 提出的基于垂直分数扩散模型的3D牙科种植体修复方法能够有效减少CBCT图像中的伪影，提高图像质量和诊断准确性，为临床成像提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) is a widely used 3D imaging technique in dentistry, offering high-resolution images while minimising radiation exposure for patients. However, CBCT is highly susceptible to artefacts arising from high-density objects such as dental implants, which can compromise image quality and diagnostic accuracy. To reduce artefacts, implant inpainting in the sequence of projections plays a crucial role in many artefact reduction approaches. Recently, diffusion models have achieved state-of-the-art results in image generation and have widely been applied to image inpainting tasks. However, to our knowledge, existing diffusion-based methods for implant inpainting operate on independent 2D projections. This approach neglects the correlations among individual projections, resulting in inconsistencies in the reconstructed images. To address this, we propose a 3D dental implant inpainting approach based on perpendicular score-based diffusion models, each trained in two different planes and operating in the projection domain. The 3D distribution of the projection series is modelled by combining the two 2D score-based diffusion models in the sampling scheme. Our results demonstrate the method's effectiveness in producing high-quality, artefact-reduced 3D CBCT images, making it a promising solution for improving clinical imaging.

</details>


### [98] [WMoE-CLIP: Wavelet-Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts Prompt Learning for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06313)
*Peng Chen,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于小波增强的专家混合提示学习方法，用于零样本异常检测，通过变分自编码器建模全局语义、小波分解提取多频特征、专家混合模块聚合上下文信息，在14个工业与医学数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法依赖固定文本提示，难以捕捉复杂语义，且仅关注空间域特征，限制了检测细微异常的能力。需要解决这些限制以提升零样本异常检测性能。

Method: 提出小波增强的专家混合提示学习方法：1) 使用变分自编码器建模全局语义表示并集成到提示中；2) 通过小波分解提取多频图像特征，通过跨模态交互动态优化文本嵌入；3) 引入语义感知的专家混合模块聚合上下文信息。

Result: 在14个工业和医学数据集上进行了广泛实验，证明了所提方法的有效性，能够更好地检测复杂和细微的异常模式。

Conclusion: 该方法通过集成全局语义建模、多频特征提取和上下文聚合，显著提升了零样本异常检测的性能，特别是在处理复杂语义和细微异常方面表现出色。

Abstract: Vision-language models have recently shown strong generalization in zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD), enabling the detection of unseen anomalies without task-specific supervision. However, existing approaches typically rely on fixed textual prompts, which struggle to capture complex semantics, and focus solely on spatial-domain features, limiting their ability to detect subtle anomalies. To address these challenges, we propose a wavelet-enhanced mixture-of-experts prompt learning method for ZSAD. Specifically, a variational autoencoder is employed to model global semantic representations and integrate them into prompts to enhance adaptability to diverse anomaly patterns. Wavelet decomposition extracts multi-frequency image features that dynamically refine textual embeddings through cross-modal interactions. Furthermore, a semantic-aware mixture-of-experts module is introduced to aggregate contextual information. Extensive experiments on 14 industrial and medical datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [99] [P-SLCR: Unsupervised Point Cloud Semantic Segmentation via Prototypes Structure Learning and Consistent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06321)
*Lixin Zhan,Jie Jiang,Tianjian Zhou,Yukun Du,Yan Zheng,Xuehu Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P-SLCR方法，一种基于原型库的无监督点云语义分割策略，通过结构学习和一致性推理实现无标注点云场景的语义分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前点云语义分割方法严重依赖人工标注，而无监督点云学习方法仍处于早期阶段。由于缺乏标注信息和预训练，无监督点云学习面临重大挑战，需要开发有效策略。

Method: 提出P-SLCR方法：1) 一致性结构学习：通过选择高质量特征，在一致性点和一致性原型库之间建立结构特征学习；2) 语义关系一致性推理：分别构建一致性和模糊性原型库之间的原型相互关系矩阵，通过该矩阵对两种原型库施加约束以保持语义一致性。

Result: 在S3DIS、SemanticKITTI和Scannet数据集上进行了广泛评估，相比无监督方法取得了最佳性能。在S3DIS数据集Area-5上实现了47.1%的mIoU，超过了经典全监督方法PointNet的2.5%。

Conclusion: P-SLCR方法通过原型库驱动的结构学习和一致性推理，有效解决了无监督点云语义分割的挑战，在多个数据集上表现出色，甚至在某些指标上超越了全监督方法。

Abstract: Current semantic segmentation approaches for point cloud scenes heavily rely on manual labeling, while research on unsupervised semantic segmentation methods specifically for raw point clouds is still in its early stages. Unsupervised point cloud learning poses significant challenges due to the absence of annotation information and the lack of pre-training. The development of effective strategies is crucial in this context. In this paper, we propose a novel prototype library-driven unsupervised point cloud semantic segmentation strategy that utilizes Structure Learning and Consistent Reasoning (P-SLCR). First, we propose a Consistent Structure Learning to establish structural feature learning between consistent points and the library of consistent prototypes by selecting high-quality features. Second, we propose a Semantic Relation Consistent Reasoning that constructs a prototype inter-relation matrix between consistent and ambiguous prototype libraries separately. This process ensures the preservation of semantic consistency by imposing constraints on consistent and ambiguous prototype libraries through the prototype inter-relation matrix. Finally, our method was extensively evaluated on the S3DIS, SemanticKITTI, and Scannet datasets, achieving the best performance compared to unsupervised methods. Specifically, the mIoU of 47.1% is achieved for Area-5 of the S3DIS dataset, surpassing the classical fully supervised method PointNet by 2.5%.

</details>


### [100] [WorldCache: Accelerating World Models for Free via Heterogeneous Token Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06331)
*Weilun Feng,Guoxin Fan,Haotong Qin,Chuanguang Yang,Mingqiang Wu,Yuqi Li,Xiangqi Li,Zhulin An,Libo Huang,Dingrui Wang,Longlong Liao,Michele Magno,Yongjun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldCache：针对扩散世界模型的缓存框架，通过曲率引导的异构令牌预测和混沌优先自适应跳过策略，实现3.7倍加速同时保持98%的推演质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散世界模型虽然具有强大的统一世界模拟能力，但其迭代去噪过程计算成本过高，难以支持交互式使用和长时域推演。现有特征缓存方法虽能加速推理，但为单模态扩散设计的策略无法有效迁移到世界模型，主要面临两个特定障碍：多模态耦合和空间变化导致的令牌异质性，以及非均匀时间动态性（少数困难令牌驱动误差增长）

Method: 提出WorldCache框架，包含两个核心技术：1) 曲率引导的异构令牌预测：使用基于物理的曲率分数估计令牌可预测性，对混沌令牌（方向突变）应用Hermite引导的阻尼预测器；2) 混沌优先自适应跳过：累积曲率归一化的无量纲漂移信号，仅在瓶颈令牌开始漂移时重新计算

Result: 在扩散世界模型上的实验表明，WorldCache实现了高达3.7倍的端到端加速，同时保持98%的推演质量，在资源受限场景下展现出显著优势和实用性

Conclusion: WorldCache是针对扩散世界模型量身定制的缓存框架，有效解决了令牌异质性和非均匀时间动态性问题，在保持高质量推演的同时大幅提升了计算效率，为资源受限场景下的世界模型应用提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Diffusion-based world models have shown strong potential for unified world simulation, but the iterative denoising remains too costly for interactive use and long-horizon rollouts. While feature caching can accelerate inference without training, we find that policies designed for single-modal diffusion transfer poorly to world models due to two world-model-specific obstacles: \emph{token heterogeneity} from multi-modal coupling and spatial variation, and \emph{non-uniform temporal dynamics} where a small set of hard tokens drives error growth, making uniform skipping either unstable or overly conservative. We propose \textbf{WorldCache}, a caching framework tailored to diffusion world models. We introduce \textit{Curvature-guided Heterogeneous Token Prediction}, which uses a physics-grounded curvature score to estimate token predictability and applies a Hermite-guided damped predictor for chaotic tokens with abrupt direction changes. We also design \textit{Chaotic-prioritized Adaptive Skipping}, which accumulates a curvature-normalized, dimensionless drift signal and recomputes only when bottleneck tokens begin to drift. Experiments on diffusion world models show that WorldCache delivers up to \textbf{3.7$\times$} end-to-end speedups while maintaining \textbf{98\%} rollout quality, demonstrating the vast advantages and practicality of WorldCache in resource-constrained scenarios. Our code is released in https://github.com/FofGofx/WorldCache.

</details>


### [101] [LATO: 3D Mesh Flow Matching with Structured TOpology Preserving LAtents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06357)
*Tianhao Zhao,Youjia Zhang,Hang Long,Jinshen Zhang,Wenbing Li,Yang Yang,Gongbo Zhang,Jozef Hladký,Matthias Nießner,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LATO是一种新颖的拓扑保持潜在表示方法，通过顶点位移场和稀疏体素VAE实现基于流匹配的可扩展3D网格生成，无需等值面提取或启发式网格化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D网格生成方法通常依赖等值面提取或启发式网格化，这些方法在保持拓扑结构、处理复杂几何形状和推理效率方面存在局限性。需要一种能够直接生成显式3D网格同时保持拓扑结构的可扩展方法。

Method: LATO采用顶点位移场表示网格，使用稀疏体素VAE将显式信号压缩为结构化、拓扑感知的体素潜在表示。解码器通过渐进细分和剪裁体素来实例化顶点位置，专用连接头查询体素潜在表示直接预测顶点对之间的边连接性。生成建模采用两阶段流匹配过程：首先生成结构体素，然后细化体素级拓扑特征。

Result: 与基于等值面/三角形的扩散模型和自回归生成方法相比，LATO能够生成具有复杂几何形状和良好拓扑结构的网格，同时在推理过程中具有高效率。

Conclusion: LATO提供了一种新颖的拓扑保持潜在表示方法，实现了基于流匹配的可扩展3D网格生成，无需等值面提取或启发式网格化，在几何复杂性、拓扑质量和推理效率方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce LATO, a novel topology-preserving latent representation that enables scalable, flow matching-based synthesis of explicit 3D meshes. LATO represents a mesh as a Vertex Displacement Field (VDF) anchored on surface, incorporating a sparse voxel Variational Autoencoder (VAE) to compress this explicit signal into a structured, topology-aware voxel latent. To decapsulate the mesh, the VAE decoder progressively subdivides and prunes latent voxels to instantiate precise vertex locations. In the end, a dedicated connection head queries the voxel latent to predict edge connectivity between vertex pairs directly, allowing mesh topology to be recovered without isosurface extraction or heuristic meshing. For generative modeling, LATO adopts a two-stage flow matching process, first synthesizing the structure voxels and subsequently refining the voxel-wise topology features. Compared to prior isosurface/triangle-based diffusion models and autoregressive generation approaches, LATO generates meshes with complex geometry, well-formed topology while being highly efficient in inference.

</details>


### [102] [OralGPT-Plus: Learning to Use Visual Tools via Reinforcement Learning for Panoramic X-ray Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06366)
*Yuxuan Fan,Jing Hao,Hong Chen,Jiahao Bao,Yihua Shao,Yuci Liang,Kuo Feng Hung,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OralGPT-Plus：用于全景牙科X光片分析的智能视觉语言模型，通过迭代对称感知诊断推理提升临床可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型采用静态单次处理范式，无法满足全景牙科X光片分析所需的细粒度空间推理、双侧对称性理解和多步诊断验证要求，限制了临床可靠性。

Method: 1. 构建DentalProbe数据集（5000张图像，含专家标注的诊断轨迹）；2. 开发OralGPT-Plus模型，采用迭代对称感知诊断推理；3. 提出Reinspection驱动的强化学习框架，结合基于规则的奖励和条件诊断驱动奖励；4. 创建MMOral-X基准测试（300个开放式问题，多难度级别区域标注）。

Result: OralGPT-Plus在MMOral-X和现有全景基准测试上持续优于强基线模型，表明交互式和对称感知推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 智能建模对牙科影像分析具有重要价值，为未来临床对齐的全景X光片分析研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Panoramic dental radiographs require fine-grained spatial reasoning, bilateral symmetry understanding, and multi-step diagnostic verification, yet existing vision-language models operate under a static single-pass paradigm that limits their clinical reliability. In this paper, we introduce OralGPT-Plus, an agentic vision-language model designed to perform iterative and symmetry-aware diagnostic reasoning for panoramic dental radiograph analysis. To support this paradigm, we construct DentalProbe, a five-thousand-image dataset with expert-curated diagnostic trajectories that provide structured supervision for localized inspection and contralateral comparison. We further develop a Reinspection-driven reinforcement learning framework that encourages clinically meaningful re-examination and stabilizes long-horizon reasoning with rubric-based reward and conditioned diagnostic-driven reward. In parallel, we present MMOral-X, the first benchmark for holistic panoramic diagnosis, containing 300 open-ended questions and region-level annotations across multiple difficulty levels. OralGPT-Plus demonstrates consistent and reliable improvements over strong baselines on MMOral-X and established panoramic benchmarks, indicating the effectiveness of interactive and symmetry-informed reasoning. Our work highlights the value of agentic modeling for dental imaging and provides a foundation for future research in clinically aligned panoramic radiograph analysis.

</details>


### [103] [Rewis3d: Reconstruction Improves Weakly-Supervised Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06374)
*Jonas Ernst,Wolfgang Boettcher,Lukas Hoyer,Jan Eric Lenssen,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Rewis3d利用前馈式3D重建技术，通过3D几何结构传播稀疏标注，显著提升2D图像弱监督语义分割性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取密集像素级标注成本高昂，稀疏标注作为弱监督替代方案存在性能差距，需要利用3D场景重建提供辅助监督信号来弥补这一差距

Method: 提出双师生架构，利用前馈式3D重建从2D视频恢复3D几何结构，在2D图像和重建的3D点云之间强制语义一致性，通过3D几何线索传播稀疏标注

Result: Rewis3d在稀疏监督设置下达到最先进性能，相比现有方法提升2-7%，无需额外标注或推理开销

Conclusion: 3D几何结构为2D弱监督语义分割提供了强大的监督信号，前馈式3D重建技术能够有效传播稀疏标注，显著提升分割性能

Abstract: We present Rewis3d, a framework that leverages recent advances in feed-forward 3D reconstruction to significantly improve weakly supervised semantic segmentation on 2D images. Obtaining dense, pixel-level annotations remains a costly bottleneck for training segmentation models. Alleviating this issue, sparse annotations offer an efficient weakly-supervised alternative. However, they still incur a performance gap. To address this, we introduce a novel approach that leverages 3D scene reconstruction as an auxiliary supervisory signal. Our key insight is that 3D geometric structure recovered from 2D videos provides strong cues that can propagate sparse annotations across entire scenes. Specifically, a dual student-teacher architecture enforces semantic consistency between 2D images and reconstructed 3D point clouds, using state-of-the-art feed-forward reconstruction to generate reliable geometric supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Rewis3d achieves state-of-the-art performance in sparse supervision, outperforming existing approaches by 2-7% without requiring additional labels or inference overhead.

</details>


### [104] [CHMv2: Improvements in Global Canopy Height Mapping using DINOv3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06382)
*John Brandt,Seungeun Yi,Jamie Tolan,Xinyuan Li,Peter Potapov,Jessica Ertel,Justine Spore,Huy V. Vo,Michaël Ramamonjisoa,Patrick Labatut,Piotr Bojanowski,Camille Couprie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHMv2是一个基于高分辨率光学卫星影像和DINOv3深度估计模型生成的全球米级分辨率冠层高度图，相比现有产品显著提高了精度并减少了偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的冠层高度信息对于量化森林碳储量、监测恢复与退化、评估栖息地结构至关重要，但全球范围内高保真的机载激光扫描(ALS)测量数据分布不均。

Method: 使用基于DINOv3构建的深度估计模型，通过高分辨率光学卫星影像生成冠层高度图；采用大规模地理多样性训练数据、自动化数据整理与配准、针对冠层高度分布定制的损失函数和数据采样策略。

Result: CHMv2相比现有产品显著提高了精度，减少了高森林的偏差，更好地保留了冠层边缘和空隙等精细尺度结构；在独立ALS测试集以及数千万个GEDI和ICESat-2观测数据上验证了其跨主要森林生物群落的一致性能。

Conclusion: CHMv2通过改进的训练数据、数据处理策略和模型设计，提供了更准确、偏差更小的全球冠层高度图，为森林监测和生态研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Accurate canopy height information is essential for quantifying forest carbon, monitoring restoration and degradation, and assessing habitat structure, yet high-fidelity measurements from airborne laser scanning (ALS) remain unevenly available globally. Here we present CHMv2, a global, meter-resolution canopy height map derived from high-resolution optical satellite imagery using a depth-estimation model built on DINOv3 and trained against ALS canopy height models. Compared to existing products, CHMv2 substantially improves accuracy, reduces bias in tall forests, and better preserves fine-scale structure such as canopy edges and gaps. These gains are enabled by a large expansion of geographically diverse training data, automated data curation and registration, and a loss formulation and data sampling strategy tailored to canopy height distributions. We validate CHMv2 against independent ALS test sets and against tens of millions of GEDI and ICESat-2 observations, demonstrating consistent performance across major forest biomes.

</details>


### [105] [REACT++: Efficient Cross-Attention for Real-Time Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06386)
*Maëlic Neau,Zoe Falomir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REACT++是基于REACT架构改进的实时场景图生成模型，通过高效特征提取和原型空间内的主体-客体交叉注意力，在保持物体检测性能的同时提升关系预测准确率，并实现20%的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前场景图生成方法通常只专注于关系预测准确性、物体检测准确性或延迟减少中的单一目标，而无法同时平衡这三个关键指标。为了支持实时应用（如具身智能体推理），需要开发能够兼顾性能与推理速度的模型。

Method: 基于REACT架构，提出REACT++模型，采用高效特征提取技术和在原型空间内实施主体到客体的交叉注意力机制，以平衡延迟和表征能力。

Result: REACT++在现有SGG模型中达到最高推理速度，相比前代REACT版本速度提升20%，关系预测准确率平均提升10%，同时不牺牲物体检测性能。

Conclusion: REACT++通过创新的架构设计成功平衡了场景图生成中的三个关键目标：关系预测准确性、物体检测准确性和推理速度，为实时应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Scene Graph Generation (SGG) is a task that encodes visual relationships between objects in images as graph structures. SGG shows significant promise as a foundational component for downstream tasks, such as reasoning for embodied agents. To enable real-time applications, SGG must address the trade-off between performance and inference speed. However, current methods tend to focus on one of the following: (1) improving relation prediction accuracy, (2) enhancing object detection accuracy, or (3) reducing latency, without aiming to balance all three objectives simultaneously. To address this limitation, we build on the powerful Real-time Efficiency and Accuracy Compromise for Tradeoffs in Scene Graph Generation (REACT) architecture and propose REACT++, a new state-of-the-art model for real-time SGG. By leveraging efficient feature extraction and subject-to-object cross-attention within the prototype space, REACT++ balances latency and representational power. REACT++ achieves the highest inference speed among existing SGG models, improving relation prediction accuracy without sacrificing object detection performance. Compared to the previous REACT version, REACT++ is 20% faster with a gain of 10% in relation prediction accuracy on average. The code is available at https://github.com/Maelic/SGG-Benchmark.

</details>


### [106] [Solving Jigsaw Puzzles in the Wild: Human-Guided Reconstruction of Cultural Heritage Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06389)
*Omidreza Safaei,Sinem Aslan,Sebastiano Vascon,Luca Palmieri,Marina Khoroshiltseva,Marcello Pelillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出人机协同拼图框架，结合自动松弛标记求解器和人工交互指导，解决大规模真实考古文物碎片重组难题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实考古文物碎片重组面临侵蚀、缺失区域、不规则形状和大规模模糊性等挑战，传统拼图求解器在RePAIR基准测试中处理数千碎片时表现不佳

Method: 提出人机协同框架，集成自动松弛标记求解器与交互式人工指导，用户可迭代锁定已验证位置、纠正错误并引导系统实现语义和几何一致的组装；引入两种互补交互策略：迭代锚定和连续交互优化

Result: 在多个RePAIR数据集上的实验表明，该混合方法在准确性和效率上显著优于全自动和手动基线方法

Conclusion: 为人机协同大规模文物重组提供了实用解决方案，通过结合自动求解器的计算能力和人类专家的领域知识，有效处理真实考古场景的复杂性

Abstract: Reassembling real-world archaeological artifacts from fragmented pieces poses significant challenges due to erosion, missing regions, irregular shapes, and large-scale ambiguity. Traditional jigsaw puzzle solvers, often designed for clean synthetic scenarios, struggle under these conditions, especially when the number of fragments grows into the thousands, as in the RePAIR benchmark. In this paper, we propose a human-in-the-loop (HIL) puzzle solving framework designed to address the complexity and scale of real-world cultural heritage reconstruction. Our approach integrates an automatic relaxation-labeling solver with interactive human guidance, allowing users to iteratively lock verified placements, correct errors, and guide the system toward semantically and geometrically coherent assemblies. We introduce two complementary interaction strategies, Iterative Anchoring and Continuous Interactive Refinement, which support scalable reconstruction across varying levels of ambiguity and puzzle size. Experiments on several RePAIR groups demonstrate that our hybrid approach substantially outperforms both fully automatic and manual baselines in accuracy and efficiency, offering a practical solution for large-scale expert-in-the-loop artifact reassembly.

</details>


### [107] [Locating and Editing Figure-Ground Organization in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06407)
*Stefan Arnold,René Gröbner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEiT视觉Transformer在感知冲突中优先选择凸性完形，研究发现这一偏好由特定注意力头（L0H9）驱动，该头在早期层引入凸性偏置，调节该头可改变完形决策边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在定位BEiT模型中实现经典格式塔凸性先验的内部组件，探究视觉Transformer如何解决由局部几何证据与全局组织先验冲突产生的感知歧义。

Method: 使用基于飞镖形状合成刺激的受控感知冲突实验，系统性地掩蔽既可支持凹性完形也可支持凸性完形的区域。通过logit归因将内部激活投影到离散视觉码本空间，分解注意力头的直接影响来识别功能单元。

Result: BEiT在感知冲突中可靠地偏好凸性完形。图-底组织在早期和中间层保持歧义，在深层突然解决。识别出注意力头L0H9作为早期种子，引入弱凸性偏置；下调该单个注意力头可连续改变感知冲突的分布质量，使凹性证据引导完形。

Conclusion: 格式塔凸性先验在BEiT中由特定可识别的功能单元实现，特别是注意力头L0H9在早期层引入凸性偏置，这一机制可通过调节单个注意力头来操纵，揭示了Transformer内部处理感知歧义的可解释机制。

Abstract: Vision Transformers must resolve figure-ground organization by choosing between completions driven by local geometric evidence and those favored by global organizational priors, giving rise to a characteristic perceptual ambiguity. We aim to locate where the canonical Gestalt prior convexity is realized within the internal components of BEiT. Using a controlled perceptual conflict based on synthetic shapes of darts, we systematically mask regions that equally admit either a concave completion or a convex completion. We show that BEiT reliably favors convex completion under this competition. Projecting internal activations into the model's discrete visual codebook space via logit attribution reveals that this preference is governed by identifiable functional units within transformer substructures. Specifically, we find that figure-ground organization is ambiguous through early and intermediate layers and resolves abruptly in later layers. By decomposing the direct effect of attention heads, we identify head L0H9 acting as an early seed, introducing a weak bias toward convexity. Downscaling this single attention head shifts the distributional mass of the perceptual conflict across a continuous decision boundary, allowing concave evidence to guide completion.

</details>


### [108] [What if? Emulative Simulation with World Models for Situated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06445)
*Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Yuheng Zhang,Junwei Zheng,Kunyu Peng,Chengzhi Wu,Chenguang Huang,Di Wen,Jiaming Zhang,Kailun Yang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WanderDream是首个用于心理探索模拟的大规模数据集，支持模型在不进行主动探索的情况下进行空间推理，包含15.8K全景视频和158K问答对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中由于机器人物理限制或视障用户安全考虑，主动探索往往不可行。需要让智能体仅凭有限观察就能心理模拟未来轨迹并回答空间假设问题。

Method: 构建WanderDream数据集：WanderDream-Gen包含1,088个真实场景的15.8K全景视频，描绘从当前视点到目标情境的想象轨迹；WanderDream-QA包含158K问答对，涵盖轨迹的起始状态、路径和结束状态。

Result: 实验表明：(1)心理探索对情境推理至关重要；(2)世界模型在WanderDream-Gen上表现优异；(3)想象力显著促进WanderDream-QA的推理；(4)WanderDream数据在真实场景中具有出色的可迁移性。

Conclusion: WanderDream为心理探索模拟提供了首个大规模基准，证明了在不进行物理探索的情况下进行空间推理的可行性，并展示了世界模型和想象力在情境推理中的重要作用。

Abstract: Situated reasoning often relies on active exploration, yet in many real-world scenarios such exploration is infeasible due to physical constraints of robots or safety concerns of visually impaired users. Given only a limited observation, can an agent mentally simulate a future trajectory toward a target situation and answer spatial what-if questions? We introduce WanderDream, the first large-scale dataset designed for the emulative simulation of mental exploration, enabling models to reason without active exploration. WanderDream-Gen comprises 15.8K panoramic videos across 1,088 real scenes from HM3D, ScanNet++, and real-world captures, depicting imagined trajectories from current viewpoints to target situations. WanderDream-QA contains 158K question-answer pairs, covering starting states, paths, and end states along each trajectory to comprehensively evaluate exploration-based reasoning. Extensive experiments with world models and MLLMs demonstrate (1) that mental exploration is essential for situated reasoning, (2) that world models achieve compelling performance on WanderDream-Gen, (3) that imagination substantially facilitates reasoning on WanderDream-QA, and (4) that WanderDream data exhibit remarkable transferability to real-world scenarios. The source code and all data will be released.

</details>


### [109] [CaTok: Taming Mean Flows for One-Dimensional Causal Image Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06449)
*Yitong Chen,Zuxuan Wu,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CaTok是一种1D因果图像分词器，通过MeanFlow解码器解决视觉自回归模型中因果建模的挑战，支持快速一步生成和高保真多步采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉分词器将2D图像块展平为非因果序列或采用启发式排序，与"下一个token预测"模式不匹配。扩散自编码器也存在缺乏因果性或引入不平衡的问题，需要真正的因果视觉分词器。

Method: 提出CaTok：1D因果图像分词器，采用MeanFlow解码器，在时间间隔内选择token并绑定到MeanFlow目标。引入REPA-A正则化，将编码器特征与视觉基础模型对齐以稳定训练。

Result: 在ImageNet重建上达到SOTA：0.75 FID、22.53 PSNR和0.674 SSIM，训练轮次更少。自回归模型性能与领先方法相当。

Conclusion: CaTok成功将因果分词范式扩展到视觉领域，学习到的因果1D表示支持快速生成和高保真采样，并能捕捉跨token间隔的多样化视觉概念。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) language models rely on causal tokenization, but extending this paradigm to vision remains non-trivial. Current visual tokenizers either flatten 2D patches into non-causal sequences or enforce heuristic orderings that misalign with the "next-token prediction" pattern. Recent diffusion autoencoders similarly fall short: conditioning the decoder on all tokens lacks causality, while applying nested dropout mechanism introduces imbalance. To address these challenges, we present CaTok, a 1D causal image tokenizer with a MeanFlow decoder. By selecting tokens over time intervals and binding them to the MeanFlow objective, as illustrated in Fig. 1, CaTok learns causal 1D representations that support both fast one-step generation and high-fidelity multi-step sampling, while naturally capturing diverse visual concepts across token intervals. To further stabilize and accelerate training, we propose a straightforward regularization REPA-A, which aligns encoder features with Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). Experiments demonstrate that CaTok achieves state-of-the-art results on ImageNet reconstruction, reaching 0.75 FID, 22.53 PSNR and 0.674 SSIM with fewer training epochs, and the AR model attains performance comparable to leading approaches.

</details>


### [110] [Pinterest Canvas: Large-Scale Image Generation at Pinterest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06453)
*Yu Wang,Eric Tzeng,Raymond Shiau,Jie Yang,Dmitry Kislyuk,Charles Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pinterest Canvas是一个大规模图像生成系统，通过基础扩散模型训练和任务特定微调，支持图像编辑和增强用例，在背景增强和宽高比外绘等任务中显著提升用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型虽然灵活，但难以通过提示或简单推理适应来控制，不适合具有严格产品要求的用例。需要构建能够支持Pinterest图像编辑和增强用例的系统。

Method: 首先在多样化多模态数据集上训练基础扩散模型，获得广泛的图像编辑能力。然后针对特定下游任务，在任务特定数据集上快速微调基础模型变体，为每个用例生成专门的模型。

Result: 在线A/B实验显示，增强图像分别获得18.0%和12.5%的显著参与度提升。与人工评分者的比较进一步验证了模型在这些任务上优于第三方模型。系统还展示了多图像场景合成和图像到视频生成等变体的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Pinterest Canvas系统通过基础模型训练和任务特定微调的方法，能够有效支持多种图像编辑和增强用例，满足严格的产品要求，并在实际应用中验证了其效果和泛化能力。

Abstract: While recent image generation models demonstrate a remarkable ability to handle a wide variety of image generation tasks, this flexibility makes them hard to control via prompting or simple inference adaptation alone, rendering them unsuitable for use cases with strict product requirements. In this paper, we introduce Pinterest Canvas, our large-scale image generation system built to support image editing and enhancement use cases at Pinterest. Canvas is first trained on a diverse, multimodal dataset to produce a foundational diffusion model with broad image-editing capabilities. However, rather than relying on one generic model to handle every downstream task, we instead rapidly fine-tune variants of this base model on task-specific datasets, producing specialized models for individual use cases. We describe key components of Canvas and summarize our best practices for dataset curation, training, and inference. We also showcase task-specific variants through case studies on background enhancement and aspect-ratio outpainting, highlighting how we tackle their specific product requirements. Online A/B experiments demonstrate that our enhanced images receive a significant 18.0% and 12.5% engagement lift, respectively, and comparisons with human raters further validate that our models outperform third-party models on these tasks. Finally, we showcase other Canvas variants, including multi-image scene synthesis and image-to-video generation, demonstrating that our approach can generalize to a wide variety of potential downstream tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Training Flow Matching: The Role of Weighting and Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06454)
*Anne Gagneux,Ségolène Martin,Rémi Gribonval,Mathurin Massias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统研究去噪生成模型的训练目标，重点关注损失权重和输出参数化（噪声、干净图像、速度），分析这些选择与数据流形内在维度、模型架构和数据集大小的相互作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究去噪生成模型的训练目标设计，特别是损失权重和输出参数化的不同形式（噪声、干净图像、速度），旨在理解这些训练选择如何与数据特性、模型架构等因素相互作用，为实际训练提供指导

Method: 通过系统数值研究，使用合成数据集（可控几何）和图像数据，比较不同训练目标，采用定量指标评估去噪精度（不同噪声水平的PSNR）和生成质量（FID）

Result: 实验揭示了训练目标选择与数据流形内在维度、模型架构、数据集大小之间的复杂相互作用，为流匹配模型的实际训练提供了设计选择的实用见解

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于去噪生成模型训练目标设计的系统性分析，揭示了损失权重和输出参数化选择与数据特性、模型架构等因素的相互作用，为流匹配模型的训练提供了实用指导

Abstract: We study the training objectives of denoising-based generative models, with a particular focus on loss weighting and output parameterization, including noise-, clean image-, and velocity-based formulations. Through a systematic numerical study, we analyze how these training choices interact with the intrinsic dimensionality of the data manifold, model architecture, and dataset size. Our experiments span synthetic datasets with controlled geometry as well as image data, and compare training objectives using quantitative metrics for denoising accuracy (PSNR across noise levels) and generative quality (FID). Rather than proposing a new method, our goal is to disentangle the various factors that matter when training a flow matching model, in order to provide practical insights on design choices.

</details>


### [112] [GreenRFM: Toward a resource-efficient radiology foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06467)
*Yingtai Li,Shuai Ming,Mingyue Zhao,Haoran Lai,Rongsheng Wang,Rui Zhou,Rundong Wang,Yujia Li,Wei Wei,Shaohua Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GreenRFM：一个资源高效的放射学基础模型预训练框架，通过MUST监督设计而非数据堆叠，在显著降低计算需求的同时达到SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学基础模型（RFMs）开发过度依赖暴力扩展，直接移植自然图像处理方法，导致模型在临床实践中脆弱且昂贵。需要资源高效的方法来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出GreenRFM框架，采用MUST（More distilled, Ubiquitous, Semantic-enforcing, and Task-aligning）监督设计原则，最大化利用监督信号而非单纯增加数据量。提供两种配置：高性能模型（24GB GPU/24小时）和轻量级模型（6GB VRAM/4小时）。

Result: 在超过20万张图像（来自4个机构、2种模态）的实验中，GreenRFM在胸部和腹部CT数据集上均超越基线模型，在公开和私有基准测试中表现优异。在内部肌肉骨骼MRI图像上的结果证明监督原则在不同模态间可迁移。

Conclusion: GreenRFM的性能和效率挑战了"规模即一切"的教条，使临床医生即使在笔记本电脑上也能公平地开发SOTA放射学基础模型，为资源高效的医学AI发展提供了新范式。

Abstract: The development of radiology foundation models (RFMs) is hindered by a reliance on brute-force scaling. Existing approaches often directly translate methods for natural images, which prioritize scale over precision and hence lead to brittle and expensive models in clinical practice. To address this, we present a resource-efficient pre-training framework, GreenRFM, that achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our framework ensures robust generalization across diverse patient populations and imaging protocols, reducing computational requirements by orders of magnitude while surpassing complex, parameter-heavy models. These capabilities stem from principled supervision design that aims to maximally utilize supervisory signals via More distilled, Ubiquitous, Semantic-enforcing, and Task-aligning (MUST) supervision, rather than simply piling up the quantity of training data. We offer two GreenRFM configurations: (i) a performant model that establishes a new state-of-the-art using a single 24GB GPU within 24 hours, and (ii) a lightweight model that matches existing benchmarks with 6GB VRAM in 4 hours. We conduct extensive experiments using over 200,000 images from four institutions and of two modalities. GreenRFMs achieve superior performances on chest and abdominal CT datasets, regardless of public or private benchmark, surpassing a range of baseline models. In addition, the results on internal musculoskeletal MRI images show that the same supervision principles transfer between different modalities. Our performance and efficiency challenge the ``scale is all you need'' dogma and democratize the equitable development of state-of-the-art RFMs for clinicians even on a laptop.

</details>


### [113] [Match4Annotate: Propagating Sparse Video Annotations via Implicit Neural Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06471)
*Zhuorui Zhang,Roger Pallarès-López,Praneeth Namburi,Brian W. Anthony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Match4Annotate：基于SIREN隐式神经表示的轻量级视频标注传播框架，支持点标注和掩码标注的帧内与跨视频传播，在临床超声数据集上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业领域（如医学影像）的视频标注成本高昂，现有标注传播方法存在局限性：视频跟踪器和分割模型需要逐视频初始化且无法跨视频泛化；传统对应关系方法在低纹理场景表现不佳；密集特征匹配和一次性分割方法缺乏时空平滑性且不支持点标注和掩码标注的统一处理。
