While early systems relied on handcrafted features and statistical models, recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have made it possible to evaluate student writing with unprecedented flexibility. This paper investigates the application of state-of-the-art open-weight LLMs for the grading of Austrian A-level German texts, with a particular focus on rubric-based evaluation. A dataset of 101 anonymised student exams across three text types was processed and evaluated. Four LLMs, DeepSeek-R1 32b, Qwen3 30b, Mixtral 8x7b and LLama3.3 70b, were evaluated with different contexts and prompting strategies. The LLMs were able to reach a maximum of 40.6% agreement with the human rater in the rubric-provided sub-dimensions, and only 32.8% of final grades matched the ones given by a human expert. The results indicate that even though smaller models are able to use standardised rubrics for German essay grading, they are not accurate enough to be used in a real-world grading environment.

</details>


### [151] [Experiences Build Characters: The Linguistic Origins and Functional Impact of LLM Personality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06088)
*Xi Wang,Mengdie Zhuang,Jiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过持续预训练让语言模型接触特定领域文本，模拟经验积累，发现模型能力呈双峰分布，存在"表达型通才"和"抑制型专才"两种最优类型，并揭示了"抑制优势"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类问题解决受益于多样化的风格和人格特质，但当前大型语言模型的发展主要关注统一性能基准，偏向特定的行为倾向如自信性。研究旨在探索不同经验如何塑造机器个性并影响问题解决能力。

Method: 采用持续预训练方法，以无监督方式让模型接触领域特定文本，模拟经验积累过程。通过机器人格量表(MPI)调整大五人格框架，量化模型变体的人格特质，并分析其与语言风格和推理行为的关系。

Result: 发现模型能力呈双峰分布，峰值出现在"表达型通才"和"抑制型专才"两种类型。识别出"抑制优势"现象，即降低社交特质能提升复杂推理性能。建立了训练数据语言学特征(如祈使句频率、词汇多样性)与模型人格之间的因果关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为"人格工程学"提供了路线图，表明可以通过控制训练数据的语言学特征来设计特定人格特质的语言模型，从而优化不同任务的问题解决能力。

Abstract: Human problem-solving is enriched by a diversity of styles and personality traits, yet the development of Large Language Models (LLMs) has largely prioritized uniform performance benchmarks that favour specific behavioural tendencies such as assertiveness. To investigate how diverse experiences shape machine personality and influence problem-solving, this study employs continued pre-training to expose models to domain-specific texts in an unsupervised manner, simulating the accumulation of experience. By adapting the Big Five framework via the Machine Personality Inventory (MPI), we quantify the personality traits of these model variants and analyse their relationship to linguistic style and reasoning behaviour. The findings reveal that model competence is bimodal, peaking at "Expressive Generalists" and "Suppressed Specialists," while identifying a "Suppression Advantage" where reduced social traits enhance complex reasoning performance. This study further establishes a causal link between training data linguistics, such as imperative frequency, and lexical diversity, providing a roadmap for "Personality Engineering".

</details>


### [152] [Making Implicit Premises Explicit in Logical Understanding of Enthymemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06114)
*Xuyao Feng,Anthony Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个集成LLM和神经符号推理的管道，用于将自然语言中的省略三段论转换为逻辑论证并确定逻辑蕴涵关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的文本和对话论证通常是省略三段论（部分前提或主张是隐含的）。现有的NLP方法只能识别省略三段论但不能解码其底层逻辑，而基于逻辑的方法需要充足的知识库公式进行溯因推理。缺乏将省略三段论文本组件转换为逻辑论证并生成解码所需逻辑公式的系统方法。

Method: 提出一个三阶段管道：1) 使用LLM基于显式前提和主张生成中间隐含前提；2) 使用另一个LLM将自然语言翻译为逻辑公式；3) 使用基于SAT求解器的神经符号推理器确定蕴涵关系。

Result: 在两个省略三段论数据集上评估管道，在精确率、召回率、F1分数和准确率方面显示出有前景的性能，能够正确选择隐含前提。

Conclusion: 该管道成功整合了LLM的自然语言处理能力和符号推理的逻辑严谨性，为处理自然语言中的省略三段论提供了一种系统方法，填补了现有方法的空白。

Abstract: Real-world arguments in text and dialogues are normally enthymemes (i.e. some of their premises and/or claims are implicit). Natural language processing (NLP) methods for handling enthymemes can potentially identify enthymemes in text but they do not decode their underlying logic, whereas logic-based approaches for handling them assume a knowledgebase with sufficient formulae that can be used to decode them via abduction. There is therefore a lack of a systematic method for translating textual components of an enthymeme into a logical argument and generating the logical formulae required for their decoding, and thereby showing logical entailment. To address this, we propose a pipeline that integrates: (1) a large language model (LLM) to generate intermediate implicit premises based on the explicit premise and claim; (2) another LLM to translate the natural language into logical formulas; and (3) a neuro-symbolic reasoner based on a SAT solver to determine entailment. We evaluate our pipeline on two enthymeme datasets, demonstrating promising performance in selecting the correct implicit premise, as measured by precision, recall, F1-score, and accuracy.

</details>


### [153] [A Causal Graph Approach to Oppositional Narrative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06135)
*Diego Revilla,Martin Fernandez-de-Retana,Lingfeng Chen,Aritz Bilbao-Jayo,Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于图的方法检测对立叙事，将叙事表示为实体交互图，通过因果估计构建最小因果子图，优于现有对立思维分类方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本分析方法依赖预定义本体论标注的数据，常将人类偏见嵌入黑盒模型，且利用非结构化线性模式识别而非建模实体间的结构化交互

Method: 提出基于图的框架，将叙事表示为实体交互图，在节点层面融入因果估计，将构建的句子图提炼为最小因果子图，并基于此表示构建分类流程

Result: 该方法在对立思维分类任务中优于现有方法

Conclusion: 图表示结合因果估计能更好地检测和分析对立叙事，提供结构化交互建模和因果解释能力

Abstract: Current methods for textual analysis rely on data annotated within predefined ontologies, often embedding human bias within black-box models. Despite achieving near-perfect performance, these approaches exploit unstructured, linear pattern recognition rather than modeling the structured interactions between entities that naturally emerge in discourse. In this work, we propose a graph-based framework for the detection, analysis, and classification of oppositional narratives and their underlying entities by representing narratives as entity-interaction graphs. Moreover, by incorporating causal estimation at the node level, our approach derives a causal representation of each contribution to the final classification by distilling the constructed sentence graph into a minimal causal subgraph. Building upon this representation, we introduce a classification pipeline that outperforms existing approaches to oppositional thinking classification task.

</details>


### [154] [CRIMSON: A Clinically-Grounded LLM-Based Metric for Generative Radiology Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06183)
*Mohammed Baharoon,Thibault Heintz,Siavash Raissi,Mahmoud Alabbad,Mona Alhammad,Hassan AlOmaish,Sung Eun Kim,Oishi Banerjee,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRIMSON是一个基于临床的胸部X光报告生成评估框架，通过诊断正确性、上下文相关性和患者安全性三个维度进行评估，整合完整临床上下文并防止正常或临床不显著发现对评分产生不当影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标未能充分整合临床上下文（如患者年龄、适应症、指南决策规则），且常让正常或临床不显著发现对总体评分产生不当影响，缺乏对临床重要错误的优先考虑。

Method: 开发了一个全面的错误分类法，涵盖假阳性发现、遗漏发现和八种属性级错误；每个发现根据与心胸放射科医师合作制定的指南分配临床显著性级别（紧急、可行动非紧急、不可行动、预期/良性）；采用严重性感知加权，优先考虑临床后果严重的错误。

Result: 在ReXVal中与六名认证放射科医师标注的临床显著错误计数高度一致（Kendall's tau = 0.61-0.71；Pearson's r = 0.71-0.84）；在RadJudge（临床挑战性通过-失败场景）中与专家判断一致；在RadPref（超过100对病例的放射科医师偏好基准）中与放射科医师偏好最强对齐。

Conclusion: CRIMSON提供了一个临床基础、可重复的胸部X光报告生成评估框架，通过整合完整临床上下文、严重性感知加权和全面错误分类，实现了与放射科医师判断和偏好的强一致性，并发布了相关基准和模型。

Abstract: We introduce CRIMSON, a clinically grounded evaluation framework for chest X-ray report generation that assesses reports based on diagnostic correctness, contextual relevance, and patient safety. Unlike prior metrics, CRIMSON incorporates full clinical context, including patient age, indication, and guideline-based decision rules, and prevents normal or clinically insignificant findings from exerting disproportionate influence on the overall score. The framework categorizes errors into a comprehensive taxonomy covering false findings, missing findings, and eight attribute-level errors (e.g., location, severity, measurement, and diagnostic overinterpretation). Each finding is assigned a clinical significance level (urgent, actionable non-urgent, non-actionable, or expected/benign), based on a guideline developed in collaboration with attending cardiothoracic radiologists, enabling severity-aware weighting that prioritizes clinically consequential mistakes over benign discrepancies. CRIMSON is validated through strong alignment with clinically significant error counts annotated by six board-certified radiologists in ReXVal (Kendalls tau = 0.61-0.71; Pearsons r = 0.71-0.84), and through two additional benchmarks that we introduce. In RadJudge, a targeted suite of clinically challenging pass-fail scenarios, CRIMSON shows consistent agreement with expert judgment. In RadPref, a larger radiologist preference benchmark of over 100 pairwise cases with structured error categorization, severity modeling, and 1-5 overall quality ratings from three cardiothoracic radiologists, CRIMSON achieves the strongest alignment with radiologist preferences. We release the metric, the evaluation benchmarks, RadJudge and RadPref, and a fine-tuned MedGemma model to enable reproducible evaluation of report generation, all available at https://github.com/rajpurkarlab/CRIMSON.

</details>


### [155] [MAPO: Mixed Advantage Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06194)
*Naifan Zhang,Ruihan Sun,Jinwei Su,Hengjie Yang,Zhengyuan Pan,Zhaohan Chen,Xiaofan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAPO：一种用于主观多轮对话的无critic高效RL算法，利用法官模型的密集过程反馈和蒙特卡洛回报传播长期效应，通过混合优势估计器实现细粒度可扩展的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观多轮对话任务（如情感支持）需要适应动态用户状态并优化长期交互质量的对话策略。然而，由于缺乏可靠的过程监督，强化学习在此类场景中面临挑战：仅基于结果的训练将多轮信用分配压缩为单一轨迹级奖励，而简单的轮级组采样在交互环境中产生过高成本。

Method: 提出MAPO算法：1）利用法官模型提供密集过程反馈；2）通过蒙特卡洛回报传播长期效应；3）引入混合优势估计器，结合轮级归一化和批次级归一化，实现细粒度且可扩展的信用分配；4）无critic设计提高效率。

Result: 在多个主观对话基准测试（EMPA、EmoBench、EQ-Bench）和7B到32B模型规模上，MAPO相比仅基于结果的GRPO和单级归一化基线，显著提升训练稳定性和最终性能。在EMPA上，成功率提升高达9个百分点，对话分数比7B基础模型提高+43.2。尽管仅在EMPA风格环境中训练，方法在未见的情感智能基准上泛化良好，EmoBench提升高达+4分，EQ-Bench提升+3.5分。

Conclusion: 密集过程监督与混合级归一化相结合，能够实现主观、开放式多轮对话的有效且可扩展的强化学习。MAPO算法通过精细的信用分配和高效的训练机制，解决了长期交互中的过程监督和优化稳定性问题。

Abstract: Subjective multi-turn dialogue tasks, such as emotional support, require conversational policies that adapt to evolving user states and optimize long-horizon interaction quality. However, reinforcement learning (RL) for such settings remains challenging due to the absence of reliable process supervision. Outcome-only training collapses credit assignment across turns into a single trajectory-level reward, while naïve turn-level group sampling incurs prohibitive rollout costs in interactive environments. We propose a critic-free and efficient RL algorithm named MAPO that leverages dense process feedback from a judge model and propagates long-horizon effects through Monte Carlo returns. To stabilize optimization, we introduce a mixed advantage estimator that combines turn-level normalization with batch-level normalization, enabling fine-grained yet scalable credit assignment. Across multiple subjective dialogue benchmarks, including EMPA, EmoBench, and EQ-Bench, and model scales ranging from 7B to 32B, our method consistently improves both training stability and final performance over outcome-only GRPO and single-level normalization baselines. On EMPA, we improve rates by up to 9 points and increase dialogue scores by as much as +43.2 over the 7B base model. Despite training only on EMPA-style environments, our approach generalizes well, yielding consistent improvements on unseen emotional-intelligence benchmarks, including up to +4 points on EmoBench and +3.5 on EQ-Bench. Together, these results demonstrate that dense process supervision combined with mixed-level normalization enables effective and scalable RL for subjective, open-ended multi-turn dialogue.

</details>


### [156] [Wisdom of the AI Crowd (AI-CROWD) for Ground Truth Approximation in Content Analysis: A Research Protocol & Validation Using Eleven Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06197)
*Luis de-Marcos,Manuel Goyanes,Adrián Domínguez-Díaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AI-CROWD协议利用多个大语言模型的集体输出来近似真实标注，通过多数投票和诊断指标分析一致性/分歧模式，识别高置信度分类并标记潜在模糊性或模型特定偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模内容分析面临缺乏可观测真实标注的挑战，因为通过人工编码创建大规模基准数据集在时间、成本和一致性方面都不切实际。

Method: 引入AI-CROWD协议，利用大语言模型集合的集体输出来近似真实标注，通过多数投票聚合输出，并使用诊断指标分析一致性和分歧模式。

Result: 协议能够识别高置信度分类，同时标记潜在模糊性或模型特定偏差，为大规模内容分析提供实用的标注近似方法。

Conclusion: AI-CROWD协议为缺乏真实标注的大规模内容分析提供了一种可行的解决方案，通过模型集合的共识来近似真实标注，同时识别不确定性来源。

Abstract: Large-scale content analysis is increasingly limited by the absence of observable ground truth or gold-standard labels, as creating such benchmarks through extensive human coding becomes impractical for massive datasets due to high time, cost, and consistency challenges. To overcome this barrier, we introduce the AI-CROWD protocol, which approximates ground truth by leveraging the collective outputs of an ensemble of large language models (LLMs). Rather than asserting that the resulting labels are true ground truth, the protocol generates a consensus-based approximation derived from convergent and divergent inferences across multiple models. By aggregating outputs via majority voting and interrogating agreement/disagreement patterns with diagnostic metrics, AI-CROWD identifies high-confidence classifications while flagging potential ambiguity or model-specific biases.

</details>


### [157] [LIT-RAGBench: Benchmarking Generator Capabilities of Large Language Models in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06198)
*Koki Itai,Shunichi Hasegawa,Yuta Yamamoto,Gouki Minegishi,Masaki Otsuki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LIT-RAGBench是一个新的RAG生成器基准测试，涵盖集成、推理、逻辑、表格和弃权五个类别，使用虚构实体和场景评估模型在统一条件下的多能力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG生成器基准测试覆盖范围有限，无法在统一条件下同时评估多种实际应用所需的能力，如长上下文证据整合、多步推理、表格解释和证据缺失时的弃权判断。

Method: 构建包含114个人工构建的日语问题及其机器翻译加人工校对的英语版本的数据集，使用虚构实体和场景确保答案基于外部文档。定义五个评估类别：集成、推理、逻辑、表格和弃权，每个类别进一步细分为实际评估方面，并系统覆盖跨类别的多模式组合。采用LLM-as-a-Judge进行评分。

Result: 在API模型和开源模型测试中，没有模型超过90%的整体准确率。基准测试能够测量每个类别内的优势和弱点，为实际RAG部署中的模型选择和构建RAG专用模型提供有价值的指标。

Conclusion: LIT-RAGBench填补了现有评估与实际应用之间的差距，通过系统覆盖多个能力类别和跨类别模式组合，为RAG生成器的综合评估提供了标准化基准，有助于推动RAG技术的实际应用和模型开发。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a framework in which a Generator, such as a Large Language Model (LLM), produces answers by retrieving documents from an external collection using a Retriever. In practice, Generators must integrate evidence from long contexts, perform multi-step reasoning, interpret tables, and abstain when evidence is missing. However, existing benchmarks for Generators provide limited coverage, with none enabling simultaneous evaluation of multiple capabilities under unified conditions. To bridge the gap between existing evaluations and practical use, we introduce LIT-RAGBench (the Logic, Integration, Table, Reasoning, and Abstention RAG Generator Benchmark), which defines five categories: Integration, Reasoning, Logic, Table, and Abstention, each further divided into practical evaluation aspects. LIT-RAGBench systematically covers patterns combining multiple aspects across categories. By using fictional entities and scenarios, LIT-RAGBench evaluates answers grounded in the provided external documents. The dataset consists of 114 human-constructed Japanese questions and an English version generated by machine translation with human curation. We use LLM-as-a-Judge for scoring and report category-wise and overall accuracy. Across API-based and open-weight models, no model exceeds 90% overall accuracy. By making strengths and weaknesses measurable within each category, LIT-RAGBench serves as a valuable metric for model selection in practical RAG deployments and for building RAG-specialized models. We release LIT-RAGBench, including the dataset and evaluation code, at https://github.com/Koki-Itai/LIT-RAGBench.

</details>


### [158] [FlashPrefill: Instantaneous Pattern Discovery and Thresholding for Ultra-Fast Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06199)
*Qihang Fan,Huaibo Huang,Zhiying Wu,Juqiu Wang,Bingning Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlashPrefill：通过瞬时模式发现和动态阈值实现超快速预填充的框架，在256K序列上实现27.78倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文建模是LLM的关键能力，但注意力机制的二次复杂度在计算密集的预填充阶段成为关键瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法存在搜索延迟高或稀疏性不足的问题。

Method: 提出FlashPrefill框架，采用快速块搜索技术同时定位动态垂直、斜线和块稀疏注意力模式，并引入动态阈值机制避免排序或累积注意力分数的开销，有效消除长尾分布以增强稀疏性。

Result: 在256K序列上实现27.78倍加速，在4K上下文长度下仍保持1.71倍加速，相比现有方法在短上下文上效率下降的问题，FlashPrefill展现了跨序列规模的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: FlashPrefill通过创新的模式发现和动态阈值技术，显著提升了长上下文建模的预填充效率，解决了现有稀疏注意力方法的局限性，为大规模序列处理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context modeling is a pivotal capability for Large Language Models, yet the quadratic complexity of attention remains a critical bottleneck, particularly during the compute-intensive prefilling phase. While various sparse attention mechanisms have been explored, they typically suffer from either significant search latency or insufficient sparsity. In this paper, we propose FlashPrefill, a framework enabling ultra-fast prefilling via instantaneous pattern discovery and thresholding. FlashPrefill leverages a fast block-searching technique to simultaneously locate dynamic vertical, slash, and block-sparse attention patterns. Crucially, it introduces a dynamic thresholding mechanism that bypasses the prohibitive overhead of sorting or accumulating attention scores while effectively eliminating the long-tail distribution to enhance sparsity. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FlashPrefill achieves a substantial leap in efficiency, delivering an unprecedented 27.78x speedup on 256K sequences. Notably, unlike existing methods that incur efficiency degradation on shorter contexts, FlashPrefill maintains a 1.71x speedup even at a 4K context length, demonstrating its robustness and practical utility across varying sequence scales.

</details>


### [159] [SPOT: Span-level Pause-of-Thought for Efficient and Interpretable Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06222)
*Yunlong Chu,Minglai Shao,Yuhang Liu,Bing Hao,Yumeng Lin,Jialu Wang,Ruijie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPOT框架通过将显式思维链压缩为紧凑的潜在暂停令牌，使用跨层语义对齐和冻结头部解码约束，在减少37.5%生成令牌的同时提升推理准确率2.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式思维链推理虽然能提升大语言模型的推理性能，但会产生冗长的令牌级轨迹，导致高昂的推理成本。现有方法主要通过简洁提示或步骤剪枝来减少开销，但这些方法主要截断模型输出而非内化模型思考。潜在推理提供了有前景的替代方案，但面临两个关键挑战：1) 现有方法依赖刚性的点对点对齐，强制潜在令牌近似推理步骤的最终表示，不足以捕捉整个推理段的密集、可变长度语义；2) 缺乏可解释性，潜在状态通常通过无约束优化或嵌入混合产生，难以在预训练语言头部下解码或审计。

Method: SPOT框架包含两个核心组件：1) 跨层语义对齐：使用Sinkhorn最优传输目标，将每个暂停令牌软匹配到整个推理段的语义，克服步骤末端对齐的刚性；2) 冻结头部解码约束：保持潜在状态在冻结的预训练LM头部下可直接解码为令牌分布，实现对潜在思考的可读关键词解释。该方法不强制固定的响应模板，灵活地将显式CoT压缩为紧凑的潜在暂停令牌。

Result: 在推理基准测试中，SPOT平均提升准确率2.3个百分点，同时减少37.5%的生成令牌。该方法还提供了对潜在推理过程的忠实语义解释，增强了模型的可解释性。

Conclusion: SPOT通过跨层语义对齐和冻结头部解码约束，有效解决了潜在推理中的语义对齐刚性和可解释性不足问题，在显著减少推理成本的同时提升性能，并为潜在思考过程提供了可读的解释。

Abstract: Explicit Chain-of-Thought improves the reasoning performance of large language models but often incurs high inference cost due to verbose token-level traces. While recent approaches reduce this overhead via concise prompting or step pruning, they largely truncate what the model says rather than internalize what the model thinks. Latent reasoning offers a promising alternative by performing computation in the hidden space, yet prior methods face two critical challenges. Many existing approaches rely on rigid point-to-point alignment, forcing a latent token to approximate the final representation of a reasoning step, which can be insufficient to capture the dense, variable-length semantics of an entire reasoning segment. Furthermore, these methods often suffer from a lack of interpretability: latent states are commonly produced by unconstrained optimization or embedding mixing, yielding vectors that are difficult to decode or audit under the pretrained language head. We propose SPOT, a flexible framework that compresses explicit CoT into compact latent pause tokens without enforcing a fixed response template. At the core of SPOT is Span-level Semantic Alignment, a Sinkhorn optimal-transport objective that softly matches each pause token to the semantics of an entire reasoning segment, overcoming the rigidity of step-end alignment. To further improve interpretability, SPOT introduces a Frozen-Head Decoding Constraint that keeps latent states directly decodable as token distributions under the frozen pretrained LM head, enabling readable keyword interpretations of latent thoughts. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SPOT improves accuracy by 2.3 points on average while reducing generated tokens by 37.5% and provides faithful semantic interpretations of the latent reasoning process.

</details>


### [160] [Mind the Gap: Pitfalls of LLM Alignment with Asian Public Opinion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06264)
*Hari Shankar,Vedanta S P,Sriharini Margapuri,Debjani Mazumder,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru,Abhijnan Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对GPT-4o-Mini、Gemini-2.5-Flash、Llama 3.2、Mistral和Gemma 3等当代大语言模型在印度、东亚和东南亚地区的文化对齐性进行多语言审计，发现这些模型在宗教等敏感领域存在显著的文化偏差，特别是对少数群体的宗教观点代表性不足并放大负面刻板印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地在多语言、多文化环境中部署，但其主要依赖英语中心训练数据的特性可能导致与不同社会的多元文化价值观不匹配。研究旨在评估当代LLMs在敏感宗教领域的文化对齐性，揭示其在不同文化背景下的表现偏差。

Method: 采用多语言审计方法，对多个LLMs的内部表征进行多维度分析，使用对数概率/对数几率比较模型观点分布与真实公众态度。研究聚焦印度、东亚和东南亚地区，以宗教为切入点评估文化对齐性，并测试人口统计提示和母语提示等轻量级干预措施的效果。

Result: 主流模型在广泛社会议题上能与公众意见对齐，但在准确代表宗教观点方面持续失败，特别是对少数群体宗教观点的代表性不足，并经常放大负面刻板印象。轻量级干预措施部分缓解但无法消除这些文化差距。下游偏见基准评估（如CrowS-Pairs、IndiBias、ThaiCLI、KoBBQ）显示在敏感情境中存在持续危害和代表性不足问题。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了系统性、基于区域的审计对于确保大语言模型在全球范围内公平部署的紧迫需求。当前LLMs在文化对齐方面存在显著缺陷，特别是在宗教等敏感领域，需要更深入的文化敏感性和代表性改进措施。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in multilingual, multicultural settings, yet their reliance on predominantly English-centric training data risks misalignment with the diverse cultural values of different societies. In this paper, we present a comprehensive, multilingual audit of the cultural alignment of contemporary LLMs including GPT-4o-Mini, Gemini-2.5-Flash, Llama 3.2, Mistral and Gemma 3 across India, East Asia and Southeast Asia. Our study specifically focuses on the sensitive domain of religion as the prism for broader alignment. To facilitate this, we conduct a multi-faceted analysis of every LLM's internal representations, using log-probs/logits, to compare the model's opinion distributions against ground-truth public attitudes. We find that while the popular models generally align with public opinion on broad social issues, they consistently fail to accurately represent religious viewpoints, especially those of minority groups, often amplifying negative stereotypes. Lightweight interventions, such as demographic priming and native language prompting, partially mitigate but do not eliminate these cultural gaps. We further show that downstream evaluations on bias benchmarks (such as CrowS-Pairs, IndiBias, ThaiCLI, KoBBQ) reveal persistent harms and under-representation in sensitive contexts. Our findings underscore the urgent need for systematic, regionally grounded audits to ensure equitable global deployment of LLMs.

</details>


### [161] [The Art That Poses Back: Assessing AI Pastiches after Contemporary Artworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06324)
*Anca Dinu,Andreiana Mihail,Andra-Maria Florescu,Claudiu Creanga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探索ChatGPT的艺术视觉创造力，通过12位艺术家评估AI对原创艺术品的模仿能力，发现颜色纹理相似性与构图概念感知相似性存在显著差距，建议使用"风格转移仪表板"多指标评估而非单一风格指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人工智能在视觉艺术创作中的创造力，特别是ChatGPT是否有意模仿原创艺术品（绘画、雕塑、装置等）并生成新图像的能力，评估AI生成作品与原始艺术品的相似性和艺术价值。

Method: 邀请12位来自罗马尼亚、保加利亚、法国、奥地利和英国的艺术家，每人提供三件原创作品；使用ChatGPT生成这些作品的模仿版本；结合人类评估（艺术家评分和评论）与计算方法检测视觉和风格相似性或差异。

Result: 颜色和纹理相似性与构图、概念和感知相似性存在显著差距；艺术家认为ChatGPT的模仿作品缺乏维度感、上下文和意图感，更像是近似引用而非有价值的、能唤起情感的艺术品；建议使用多指标"风格转移仪表板"评估相似性。

Conclusion: ChatGPT在模仿当代艺术品时存在局限性，其生成的作品被原创艺术家认为缺乏艺术深度和情感共鸣；评估AI艺术模仿应采用多维度互补指标而非单一风格度量，以更全面理解AI与人类艺术创作的差异。

Abstract: This study explores artificial visual creativity, focusing on ChatGPT's ability to generate new images intentionally pastiching original artworks such as paintings, drawings, sculptures and installations. The process involved twelve artists from Romania, Bulgaria, France, Austria, and the United Kingdom, each invited to contribute with three of their artworks and to grade and comment on the AI-generated versions. The analysis combines human evaluation with computational methods aimed at detecting visual and stylistic similarities or divergences between the original works and their AI-produced renditions. The results point to a significant gap between color and texture-based similarity and compositional, conceptual, and perceptual one. Consequently, we advocate for the use of a "style transfer dashboard" of complementary metrics to evaluate the similarity between pastiches and originals, rather than using a single style metric. The artists' comments revealed limitations of ChatGPT's pastiches after contemporary artworks, which were perceived by the authors of the originals as lacking dimensionality, context, and intentional sense, and seeming more of a paraphrase or an approximate quotation rather than as a valuable, emotion-evoking artwork.

</details>


### [162] [Transparent AI for Mathematics: Transformer-Based Large Language Models for Mathematical Entity Relationship Extraction with XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06348)
*Tanjim Taharat Aurpa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将数学文本理解建模为数学实体关系抽取任务，使用Transformer模型实现99.39%的准确率，并结合SHAP可解释性分析提供模型预测的透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学文本理解具有挑战性，因为存在专业实体和复杂关系。需要自动提取数学问题中的实体关系，并为模型预测提供可解释性。

Method: 将数学问题解释建模为数学实体关系抽取任务，将操作数视为实体，运算符视为关系。应用基于Transformer的模型（特别是BERT）进行自动关系提取，并整合SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: BERT模型在数学实体关系抽取任务上达到99.39%的最高准确率。可解释性分析揭示了特定文本和数学特征如何影响关系预测，提供了特征重要性和模型行为的洞察。

Conclusion: 通过结合Transformer学习、任务特定数据集和可解释建模，该工作为MERE提供了有效且可解释的框架，支持自动化问题解决、知识图谱构建和智能教育系统的未来应用。

Abstract: Mathematical text understanding is a challenging task due to the presence of specialized entities and complex relationships between them. This study formulates mathematical problem interpretation as a Mathematical Entity Relation Extraction (MERE) task, where operands are treated as entities and operators as their relationships. Transformer-based models are applied to automatically extract these relations from mathematical text, with Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformers (BERT) achieving the best performance, reaching an accuracy of 99.39%. To enhance transparency and trust in the model's predictions, Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) is incorporated using Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP). The explainability analysis reveals how specific textual and mathematical features influence relation prediction, providing insights into feature importance and model behavior. By combining transformer-based learning, a task-specific dataset, and explainable modeling, this work offers an effective and interpretable framework for MERE, supporting future applications in automated problem solving, knowledge graph construction, and intelligent educational systems.

</details>


### [163] [Evaluation of Deontic Conditional Reasoning in Large Language Models: The Case of Wason's Selection Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06416)
*Hirohiko Abe,Kentaro Ozeki,Risako Ando,Takanobu Morishita,Koji Mineshima,Mitsuhiro Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在规范性规则下的推理表现优于描述性规则，且表现出类似人类的匹配偏误错误模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs语言能力提升，其推理能力受到关注。人类推理在特定领域（特别是规范性而非纯形式化情境）表现更好，但LLM推理的领域特异性尚未充分探索。本研究旨在系统比较LLMs在规范性（deontic）与描述性（descriptive）条件推理中的表现，并分析其错误模式是否反映人类认知偏误。

Method: 引入新的Wason选择任务数据集，明确编码道义模态以区分规范性条件句与描述性条件句。使用该数据集检验LLMs在规范性规则下的条件推理能力。进一步分析观察到的错误模式是否更符合确认偏误（寻求支持规则的证据）或匹配偏误（忽略否定并选择与规则词汇匹配的项目）。

Result: 结果显示：1) 与人类类似，LLMs在规范性规则下的推理表现更好；2) LLMs表现出类似匹配偏误的错误模式；3) LLMs的性能随规则类型系统性变化，其错误模式与人类在该范式中的已知偏误相似。

Conclusion: LLMs的推理能力在不同规则类型下存在系统性差异，其错误模式能够反映人类认知偏误（特别是匹配偏误）。这表明LLMs的推理机制在某些方面与人类相似，为理解LLMs的推理能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) advance in linguistic competence, their reasoning abilities are gaining increasing attention. In humans, reasoning often performs well in domain specific settings, particularly in normative rather than purely formal contexts. Although prior studies have compared LLM and human reasoning, the domain specificity of LLM reasoning remains underexplored. In this study, we introduce a new Wason Selection Task dataset that explicitly encodes deontic modality to systematically distinguish deontic from descriptive conditionals, and use it to examine LLMs' conditional reasoning under deontic rules. We further analyze whether observed error patterns are better explained by confirmation bias (a tendency to seek rule-supporting evidence) or by matching bias (a tendency to ignore negation and select items that lexically match elements of the rule). Results show that, like humans, LLMs reason better with deontic rules and display matching-bias-like errors. Together, these findings suggest that the performance of LLMs varies systematically across rule types and that their error patterns can parallel well-known human biases in this paradigm.

</details>


### [164] [From Prompting to Preference Optimization: A Comparative Study of LLM-based Automated Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06424)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Vu Hoang Pham,Xuan Thanh Huynh,Phuc Hong Mai,Vinh The Nguyen,Quang Nhut Huynh,Huy Tien Nguyen,Tung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对基于大语言模型的英语作为第二语言写作自动评分方法进行了首次统一实证比较，在雅思写作任务2上评估了四种主要范式，发现最佳配置（k-SFT+RAG）达到93%的F1分数，揭示了准确性-成本-鲁棒性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常孤立地考察各种基于LLM的自动作文评分技术，缺乏对它们在英语作为第二语言写作中相对优点的全面理解。为了填补这一空白，本研究旨在对主要LLM-based AES范式进行统一比较。

Method: 在统一的雅思写作任务2基准上评估了四种方法：(1)基于编码器的分类微调，(2)零样本和少样本提示，(3)指令微调和检索增强生成(RAG)，(4)监督微调结合直接偏好优化(DPO)和RAG。

Result: 结果显示不同方法在准确性、成本和鲁棒性之间存在明确的权衡关系。最佳配置（集成k-SFT和RAG）取得了最强的整体结果，F1分数达到93%。

Conclusion: 这项研究为英语作为第二语言的现代LLM-based自动作文评分策略提供了首次统一的实证比较，展示了在自动评分写作任务中的潜力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently reshaped Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet prior studies typically examine individual techniques in isolation, limiting understanding of their relative merits for English as a Second Language (L2) writing. To bridge this gap, we presents a comprehensive comparison of major LLM-based AES paradigms on IELTS Writing Task~2. On this unified benchmark, we evaluate four approaches: (i) encoder-based classification fine-tuning, (ii) zero- and few-shot prompting, (iii) instruction tuning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), and (iv) Supervised Fine-Tuning combined with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and RAG. Our results reveal clear accuracy-cost-robustness trade-offs across methods, the best configuration, integrating k-SFT and RAG, achieves the strongest overall results with F1-Score 93%. This study offers the first unified empirical comparison of modern LLM-based AES strategies for English L2, promising potential in auto-grading writing tasks. Code is public at https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/LLM_AES-EnL2

</details>


### [165] [Abductive Reasoning with Syllogistic Forms in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06428)
*Hirohiko Abe,Risako Ando,Takanobu Morishita Kentaro Ozeki,Koji Mineshima,Mitsuhiro Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨大型语言模型在溯因推理中的表现，通过将三段论数据集转换为溯因推理任务，评估LLMs是否表现出与人类相似的偏见，并探索超越形式演绎的语境化推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明LLMs和人类在推理中存在相似偏见，如拒绝与常识相悖的逻辑有效推论。但批评LLMs存在这些偏见可能不公平，因为人类推理不仅涉及形式演绎，还包括从有限信息中得出试探性结论的溯因推理。需要研究LLMs在溯因推理中的准确性，以更全面地评估其推理能力。

Method: 将三段论数据集转换为适合溯因推理的形式，用于评估最先进的LLMs在溯因推理任务中的表现。通过这种转换，研究LLMs是否在溯因推理中表现出偏见，并识别潜在的改进领域。

Result: 论文未在摘要中提供具体实验结果，但表明通过这种转换方法可以评估LLMs在溯因推理中的准确性，并识别其可能存在的偏见和改进空间。

Conclusion: 研究LLMs在溯因推理中的表现对于推进对复杂推理任务的理解和应用至关重要，为弥合机器与人类认知之间的差距提供了见解，强调了超越形式演绎的语境化推理的重要性。

Abstract: Research in AI using Large-Language Models (LLMs) is rapidly evolving, and the comparison of their performance with human reasoning has become a key concern. Prior studies have indicated that LLMs and humans share similar biases, such as dismissing logically valid inferences that contradict common beliefs. However, criticizing LLMs for these biases might be unfair, considering our reasoning not only involves formal deduction but also abduction, which draws tentative conclusions from limited information. Abduction can be regarded as the inverse form of syllogism in its basic structure, that is, a process of drawing a minor premise from a major premise and conclusion. This paper explores the accuracy of LLMs in abductive reasoning by converting a syllogistic dataset into one suitable for abduction. It aims to investigate whether the state-of-the-art LLMs exhibit biases in abduction and to identify potential areas for improvement, emphasizing the importance of contextualized reasoning beyond formal deduction. This investigation is vital for advancing the understanding and application of LLMs in complex reasoning tasks, offering insights into bridging the gap between machine and human cognition.

</details>


### [166] [PONTE: Personalized Orchestration for Natural Language Trustworthy Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06485)
*Vittoria Vineis,Matteo Silvestri,Lorenzo Antonelli,Filippo Betello,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PONTE是一个个性化、可验证的XAI框架，通过闭环验证和适应过程生成可信的自然语言解释，解决了传统XAI方法忽视用户差异和LLM幻觉的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释人工智能方法大多采用"一刀切"范式，忽视了用户在专业知识、目标和认知需求方面的差异。虽然大型语言模型可以将技术解释转化为自然语言，但存在忠实性和幻觉问题。需要开发能够适应不同用户需求并提供可靠解释的个性化XAI系统。

Method: 提出PONTE框架，将个性化建模为闭环验证和适应过程而非提示工程。包含三个核心组件：(1)低维偏好模型捕捉风格要求；(2)基于结构化XAI工件的偏好条件生成器；(3)验证模块确保数值忠实性、信息完整性和风格对齐，可选支持检索增强论证。用户反馈迭代更新偏好状态，实现快速个性化。

Result: 在医疗和金融领域的自动和人工评估显示，验证-精炼循环显著提高了完整性和风格对齐，优于无验证生成。人工研究进一步证实：预期偏好向量与感知风格高度一致；对生成随机性具有鲁棒性；获得一致积极的质量评估。

Conclusion: PONTE通过将个性化建模为闭环验证过程，解决了XAI中用户差异和LLM幻觉的挑战，实现了自适应、可靠的自然语言解释生成，为个性化可解释AI提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to enhance the transparency and accountability of machine learning systems, yet most methods follow a one-size-fits-all paradigm that neglects user differences in expertise, goals, and cognitive needs. Although Large Language Models can translate technical explanations into natural language, they introduce challenges related to faithfulness and hallucinations. To address these challenges, we present PONTE (Personalized Orchestration for Natural language Trustworthy Explanations), a human-in-the-loop framework for adaptive and reliable XAI narratives. PONTE models personalization as a closed-loop validation and adaptation process rather than prompt engineering. It combines: (i) a low-dimensional preference model capturing stylistic requirements; (ii) a preference-conditioned generator grounded in structured XAI artifacts; and (iii) verification modules enforcing numerical faithfulness, informational completeness, and stylistic alignment, optionally supported by retrieval-grounded argumentation. User feedback iteratively updates the preference state, enabling quick personalization. Automatic and human evaluations across healthcare and finance domains show that the verification-refinement loop substantially improves completeness and stylistic alignment over validation-free generation. Human studies further confirm strong agreement between intended preference vectors and perceived style, robustness to generation stochasticity, and consistently positive quality assessments.

</details>


### [167] [Speak in Context: Multilingual ASR with Speech Context Alignment via Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06505)
*Yuchen Zhang,Haralambos Mouratidis,Ravi Shekhar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种支持多语言和口音的上下文感知ASR框架，通过轻量级投影模块连接冻结的语音编码器和仅解码器语言模型，使用对比学习对齐语音与上下文表示，在11种语言和5种英语方言上实现超过5%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ASR系统大多局限于单语设置和短孤立话语，虽然上下文感知ASR有进展，但存在多语言支持有限以及语音与上下文表示缺乏原则性对齐两个关键挑战。

Method: 结合冻结的语音编码器和仅解码器语言模型，通过轻量级投影模块连接，支持对话历史和偏置词等结构化上下文提示，采用对比学习目标在共享嵌入空间中对齐语音和上下文表示。

Result: 在超过1,500小时的真实世界对话语音（涵盖11种语言和5种英语方言）上评估，上下文输入持续改善识别质量，对比对齐在不同上下文类型上提供额外增益，总体性能提升超过5%。

Conclusion: 上下文建模和跨模态对齐在多语言ASR中都很重要，提出的框架在保持预训练模型模块化的同时，支持多语言和口音，并通过对比学习改善语音与上下文的交互。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has benefited from advances in pretrained speech and language models, yet most systems remain constrained to monolingual settings and short, isolated utterances. While recent efforts in context-aware ASR show promise, two key challenges persist: limited multilingual support and the absence of principled alignment between speech and contextual representations. In this paper, we introduce a context-aware multilingual ASR framework that supports diverse languages and accents while preserving the modularity of pretrained models. Our approach combines a frozen speech encoder and a decoder-only language model via a lightweight projection module, allowing structured context prompts, including dialogue history and biasing words, to guide transcription. To improve interaction between speech and context, we employ a contrastive learning objective that aligns their representations in a shared embedding space. Evaluations on over 1,500 hours of real-world conversational speech across 11 languages and 5 English dialects show that contextual input consistently improves recognition quality. Contrastive alignment provides additional gains when applied to different context types, with an overall performance gain of over 5%. These results highlight the importance of both contextual modeling and cross-modal alignment in multilingual ASR.

</details>


### [168] [KCLarity at SemEval-2026 Task 6: Encoder and Zero-Shot Approaches to Political Evasion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06552)
*Archie Sage,Salvatore Greco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KCLarity团队参与SemEval 2026的CLARITY任务，研究政治话语中歧义和回避技术的分类，比较了两种建模方法并探索了辅助训练变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与SemEval 2026的CLARITY共享任务，该任务旨在对政治话语中的歧义和回避技术进行分类。研究需要探索有效的建模方法来处理这一具有挑战性的自然语言处理任务。

Method: 研究了两种建模方法：(1) 直接预测清晰度标签；(2) 先预测回避标签，然后通过任务分类层次结构推导清晰度。还探索了多种辅助训练变体，并在零样本设置下评估了解码器模型在回避优先方法下的表现。

Result: 两种建模方法在性能上相当。在基于编码器的模型中，RoBERTa-large在公共测试集上表现最佳；而在隐藏评估集上，零样本的GPT-5.2展现出更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 两种建模方法都能有效处理政治话语中的歧义和回避技术分类任务。不同模型在不同数据集上各有优势：RoBERTa-large在标准测试集上表现优异，而大型语言模型在零样本设置下具有更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper describes the KCLarity team's participation in CLARITY, a shared task at SemEval 2026 on classifying ambiguity and evasion techniques in political discourse. We investigate two modelling formulations: (i) directly predicting the clarity label, and (ii) predicting the evasion label and deriving clarity through the task taxonomy hierarchy. We further explore several auxiliary training variants and evaluate decoder-only models in a zero-shot setting under the evasion-first formulation. Overall, the two formulations yield comparable performance. Among encoder-based models, RoBERTa-large achieves the strongest results on the public test set, while zero-shot GPT-5.2 generalises better on the hidden evaluation set.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [RoboLayout: Differentiable 3D Scene Generation for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05522)
*Ali Shamsaddinlou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoboLayout扩展了LayoutVLM，通过集成智能体感知推理和改进的优化稳定性，生成符合物理约束且可供具身智能体交互的室内场景布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在空间推理和3D场景布局生成方面取得进展，但生成的布局往往难以满足具身智能体在物理约束室内环境中的交互需求。现有方法缺乏对智能体可达性和行动能力的考虑。

Method: 1. 在LayoutVLM基础上引入智能体感知推理，集成显式可达性约束到可微分布局优化过程；2. 采用通用的智能体抽象，可表示不同物理能力的实体（机器人、人类、动物等）；3. 提出局部细化阶段，选择性重新优化有问题的物体放置，提高收敛效率。

Result: RoboLayout保持了LayoutVLM的语义对齐和物理合理性，同时显著提升了面向智能体的室内场景生成适用性，在多样化场景配置中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: RoboLayout通过集成智能体感知约束和优化稳定性改进，成功扩展了LayoutVLM的能力，为具身智能体交互生成物理可行的室内场景布局，具有平台无关的通用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for spatial reasoning and 3D scene layout generation from open-ended language instructions. However, generating layouts that are not only semantically coherent but also feasible for interaction by embodied agents remains challenging, particularly in physically constrained indoor environments. In this paper, RoboLayout is introduced as an extension of LayoutVLM that augments the original framework with agent-aware reasoning and improved optimization stability. RoboLayout integrates explicit reachability constraints into a differentiable layout optimization process, enabling the generation of layouts that are navigable and actionable by embodied agents. Importantly, the agent abstraction is not limited to a specific robot platform and can represent diverse entities with distinct physical capabilities, such as service robots, warehouse robots, humans of different age groups, or animals, allowing environment design to be tailored to the intended agent. In addition, a local refinement stage is proposed that selectively reoptimizes problematic object placements while keeping the remainder of the scene fixed, improving convergence efficiency without increasing global optimization iterations. Overall, RoboLayout preserves the strong semantic alignment and physical plausibility of LayoutVLM while enhancing applicability to agent-centric indoor scene generation, as demonstrated by experimental results across diverse scene configurations.

</details>


### [170] [Reasoning Models Struggle to Control their Chains of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05706)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Robert McCarthy,Bruce W. Lee,He He,Ian Kivlichan,Bowen Baker,Micah Carroll,Tomek Korbak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估了大型语言模型的思维链可控性，发现当前模型在控制思维链内容方面能力有限，这为思维链监控提供了乐观前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是评估思维链监控的潜在漏洞：如果模型能够控制其思维链中表达的内容，可能会破坏思维链监控的有效性。需要测量这种"思维链可控性"能力。

Method: 提出了CoT-Control评估套件，包含要求模型在遵循特定思维链指令下解决问题的任务（如回答遗传学问题但不能使用"染色体"一词）。评估了不同模型、训练阶段、计算资源和问题难度下的可控性表现。

Result: 发现推理模型的思维链可控性显著低于输出可控性（Claude Sonnet 4.5分别为2.7% vs 61.9%）。可控性随模型规模增大而提高，但随RL训练、测试时计算量增加和问题难度提高而降低。即使给予激励或对抗性提示优化，可控性提升也不显著。

Conclusion: 目前思维链可控性不太可能成为思维链监控的失效模式，但低可控性的机制尚不明确。建议前沿实验室在未来模型中持续跟踪思维链可控性，这对维护思维链监控能力至关重要。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising tool for detecting misbehaviors and understanding the motivations of modern reasoning models. However, if models can control what they verbalize in their CoT, it could undermine CoT monitorability. To measure this undesirable capability -- CoT controllability -- we introduce the CoT-Control evaluation suite, which includes tasks that require models to solve problems while adhering to CoT instructions, e.g., reasoning about a genetics question without using the word 'chromosome'. We show that reasoning models possess significantly lower CoT controllability than output controllability; for instance, Claude Sonnet 4.5 can control its CoT only 2.7% of the time but 61.9% when controlling its final output. We also find that CoT controllability is higher for larger models and decreases with more RL training, test-time compute, and increased problem difficulty. CoT controllability failures extend even to situations in which models are given incentives (as opposed to direct requests) to evade CoT monitors, although models exhibit slightly higher controllability when they are told they are being monitored. Similarly, eliciting controllability by adversarially optimizing prompts does not meaningfully increase controllability. Our results leave us cautiously optimistic that CoT controllability is currently unlikely to be a failure mode of CoT monitorability. However, the mechanism behind low controllability is not well understood. Given its importance for maintaining CoT monitorability, we recommend that frontier labs track CoT controllability in future models.

</details>


### [171] [Evolving Medical Imaging Agents via Experience-driven Self-skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05860)
*Lin Fan,Pengyu Dai,Zhipeng Deng,Haolin Wang,Xun Gong,Yefeng Zheng,Yafei Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MACRO：一种自演进、经验增强的医学智能体，通过从已验证的执行轨迹中自主发现有效的多步骤工具序列，将其合成为可复用的复合工具，实现从静态工具组合到经验驱动工具发现的转变，提升多步骤编排准确性和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床图像解释本质上是多步骤和工具中心的，现有基于LLM的智能体系统将工具集和调用策略视为静态部署后不变，这种设计在现实世界的领域转移、跨任务和不断演变的诊断需求下表现脆弱，预定义的工具链经常退化并需要昂贵的手动重新设计。

Method: MACRO从已验证的执行轨迹中自主识别反复出现的有效多步骤工具序列，将其合成为可复用的复合工具，并注册为新的高级原语。采用轻量级图像特征记忆将工具选择基于视觉-临床上下文，通过GRPO-like训练循环强化发现的复合工具的可靠调用，实现最小监督下的闭环自我改进。

Result: 在多种医学影像数据集和任务上的广泛实验表明，自主复合工具发现持续提高了多步骤编排准确性和跨域泛化能力，优于强基线方法和最近最先进的智能体方法，弥合了脆弱的静态工具使用与自适应、上下文感知的临床AI辅助之间的差距。

Conclusion: MACRO通过经验驱动的工具发现和合成，实现了医学智能体的自演进能力，从静态工具组合转向动态工具发现，显著提升了临床图像解释任务中的多步骤编排性能和跨域适应性，为构建更鲁棒、自适应的临床AI辅助系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Clinical image interpretation is inherently multi-step and tool-centric: clinicians iteratively combine visual evidence with patient context, quantify findings, and refine their decisions through a sequence of specialized procedures. While LLM-based agents promise to orchestrate such heterogeneous medical tools, existing systems treat tool sets and invocation strategies as static after deployment. This design is brittle under real-world domain shifts, across tasks, and evolving diagnostic requirements, where predefined tool chains frequently degrade and demand costly manual re-design. We propose MACRO, a self-evolving, experience-augmented medical agent that shifts from static tool composition to experience-driven tool discovery. From verified execution trajectories, the agent autonomously identifies recurring effective multi-step tool sequences, synthesizes them into reusable composite tools, and registers these as new high-level primitives that continuously expand its behavioral repertoire. A lightweight image-feature memory grounds tool selection in a visual-clinical context, while a GRPO-like training loop reinforces reliable invocation of discovered composites, enabling closed-loop self-improvement with minimal supervision. Extensive experiments across diverse medical imaging datasets and tasks demonstrate that autonomous composite tool discovery consistently improves multi-step orchestration accuracy and cross-domain generalization over strong baselines and recent state-of-the-art agentic methods, bridging the gap between brittle static tool use and adaptive, context-aware clinical AI assistance. Code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [172] [The World Won't Stay Still: Programmable Evolution for Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05910)
*Guangrui Li,Yaochen Xie,Yi Liu,Ziwei Dong,Xingyuan Pan,Tianqi Zheng,Jason Choi,Michael J. Morais,Binit Jha,Shaunak Mishra,Bingrou Zhou,Chen Luo,Monica Xiao Cheng,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProEvolve：基于图的可编程环境演化框架，用于评估LLM智能体在动态环境中的适应性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试假设静态环境，忽略了现实世界的演化特性，无法有效评估智能体对环境变化的适应能力。需要一种可扩展、可控的环境演化方法来更好地评估智能体在动态现实世界中的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出ProEvolve框架，使用类型化关系图统一表示环境（数据、工具、模式）。通过图变换实现环境演化（添加、删除、修改能力），并自动生成演化环境。通过子图采样和编程实例化任务沙箱。

Result: 成功将单个环境演化为200个环境和3,000个任务沙箱，并据此对代表性智能体进行了基准测试，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: ProEvolve提供了一种可编程、可扩展的环境演化方法，能够更好地评估LLM智能体在动态现实世界中的适应性和鲁棒性，解决了现有静态基准测试的局限性。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents fulfill user requests by interacting with environments, querying data, and invoking tools in a multi-turn process. Yet, most existing benchmarks assume static environments with fixed schemas and toolsets, neglecting the evolutionary nature of the real world and agents' robustness to environmental changes. In this paper, we study a crucial problem: how to evolve the agent environment in a scalable and controllable way, thereby better evaluating agents' adaptability to real-world dynamics. We propose ProEvolve, a graph-based framework that makes environment evolution programmable. At its core, a typed relational graph provides a unified, explicit representation of the environment: data, tools, and schema. Under this formalism, adding, removing, or modifying capabilities are expressed as graph transformations that coherently propagate updates across tools, schemas, and data access. Building on this, ProEvolve can (1) program the evolutionary dynamics as graph transformations to generate environments automatically, and (2) instantiate task sandboxes via subgraph sampling and programming. We validate ProEvolve by evolving a single environment into 200 environments and 3,000 task sandboxes, and benchmark representative agents accordingly.

</details>


### [173] [Aggregative Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06067)
*Yann Munro,Isabelle Bloch,Marie-Jeanne Lesot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的聚合语义家族，用于定量双极论证框架，通过将攻击者和支持者分别聚合的三阶段计算来保持双极性，相比现有方法更具可参数化和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定量双极论证框架中的渐进语义在处理攻击者和支持者时通常采用对称方式，但在实际情境中攻击者和支持者可能扮演不对称角色。需要一种更灵活、可解释的方法来分别处理攻击和支持的影响。

Method: 提出聚合语义家族，采用三阶段计算：1) 计算攻击者的全局权重；2) 计算支持者的全局权重；3) 将这两个值与论证的内在权重聚合。讨论了三个聚合函数在不同情境下应满足的性质及其与经典渐进语义原则的关系。

Result: 通过简单示例和最终示例测试比较了500种聚合语义，展示了聚合语义可能的行为范围。三阶段分解使计算更具可参数化，保持了比文献中更进一步的双极性，并产生了更易理解的渐进语义。

Conclusion: 聚合语义为定量双极论证框架提供了一种新颖的渐进语义方法，通过分别处理攻击者和支持者的三阶段计算，实现了更好的可参数化、可解释性和对不对称角色的适应性。

Abstract: Formal argumentation is being used increasingly in artificial intelligence as an effective and understandable way to model potentially conflicting pieces of information, called arguments, and identify so-called acceptable arguments depending on a chosen semantics. This paper deals with the specific context of Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAF), where arguments have intrinsic weights and can attack or support each other. In this context, we introduce a novel family of gradual semantics, called aggregative semantics. In order to deal with situations in which attackers and supporters do not play a symmetric role, and in contrast to modular semantics, we propose to aggregate attackers and supporters separately. This leads to a three-stage computation, which consists in computing a global weight for attackers and another for supporters, before aggregating these two values with the intrinsic weight of the argument. We discuss the properties that the three aggregation functions should satisfy depending on the context, as well as their relationships with the classical principles for gradual semantics. This discussion is supported by various simple examples, as well as a final example on which five hundred aggregative semantics are tested and compared, illustrating the range of possible behaviours with aggregative semantics. Decomposing the computation into three distinct and interpretable steps leads to a more parametrisable computation: it keeps the bipolarity one step further than what is done in the literature, and it leads to more understandable gradual semantics.

</details>


### [174] [Offline Materials Optimization with CliqueFlowmer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06082)
*Jakub Grudzien Kuba,Benjamin Kurt Miller,Sergey Levine,Pieter Abbeel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CliqueFlowmer模型，将基于团的模型优化(MBO)融入Transformer和流生成，用于计算材料发现中的目标属性优化，优于传统生成方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的生成建模方法在材料发现中因最大似然训练而无法有效探索材料空间中有吸引力的区域，需要将目标属性直接优化融入生成过程

Method: 提出CliqueFlowmer模型，将基于团的模型优化(MBO)技术融入Transformer和流生成架构，实现材料属性的直接优化

Result: CliqueFlowmer在优化能力上表现优异，生成的材料性能显著优于生成式基线方法

Conclusion: CliqueFlowmer为计算材料发现提供了一种有效的离线模型优化方法，通过开源代码支持专业材料优化问题和跨学科研究

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning inspired neural network-based approaches to computational materials discovery (CMD). A plethora of problems in this field involve finding materials that optimize a target property. Nevertheless, the increasingly popular generative modeling methods are ineffective at boldly exploring attractive regions of the materials space due to their maximum likelihood training. In this work, we offer an alternative CMD technique based on offline model-based optimization (MBO) that fuses direct optimization of a target material property into generation. To that end, we introduce a domain-specific model, dubbed CliqueFlowmer, that incorporates recent advances of clique-based MBO into transformer and flow generation. We validate CliqueFlowmer's optimization abilities and show that materials it produces strongly outperform those provided by generative baselines. To enable employment of CliqueFlowmer in specialized materials optimization problems and support interdisciplinary research, we open-source our code at https://github.com/znowu/CliqueFlowmer.

</details>


### [175] [Artificial Intelligence for Climate Adaptation: Reinforcement Learning for Climate Change-Resilient Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06278)
*Miguel Costa,Arthur Vandervoort,Carolin Schmidt,João Miranda,Morten W. Petersen,Martin Drews,Karyn Morrisey,Francisco C. Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的决策支持框架，用于城市洪水适应规划，通过整合降雨预测、洪水建模、交通模拟和影响量化，学习平衡投资成本与避免影响的适应性策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧降雨和洪水，导致城市交通系统中断增加。制定有效适应策略面临挑战：基础设施投资长期性、气候不确定性、洪水-基础设施-交通复杂交互作用。

Method: 提出强化学习决策支持框架，作为综合评估模型，整合降雨预测与洪水建模、交通模拟、基础设施和交通直接间接影响量化。RL方法学习平衡投资维护成本与避免影响的适应性策略。

Result: 在哥本哈根内城2024-2100年案例研究中，测试多种适应选项及不同信念和实际气候情景。框架优于传统优化方法，发现协调的空间时间适应路径，学习影响减少与适应投资的权衡，产生更具韧性的策略。

Conclusion: 强化学习作为灵活决策支持工具，在气候不确定性下进行适应性基础设施规划具有潜力，能够发现协调的时空适应路径并学习投资与影响的权衡。

Abstract: Climate change is expected to intensify rainfall and, consequently, pluvial flooding, leading to increased disruptions in urban transportation systems over the coming decades. Designing effective adaptation strategies is challenging due to the long-term, sequential nature of infrastructure investments, deep climate uncertainty, and the complex interactions between flooding, infrastructure, and mobility impacts. In this work, we propose a novel decision-support framework using reinforcement learning (RL) for long-term flood adaptation planning. Formulated as an integrated assessment model (IAM), the framework combines rainfall projection and flood modeling, transport simulation, and quantification of direct and indirect impacts on infrastructure and mobility. Our RL-based approach learns adaptive strategies that balance investment and maintenance costs against avoided impacts. We evaluate the framework through a case study of Copenhagen's inner city over the 2024-2100 period, testing multiple adaptation options, and different belief and realized climate scenarios. Results show that the framework outperforms traditional optimization approaches by discovering coordinated spatial and temporal adaptation pathways and learning trade-offs between impact reduction and adaptation investment, yielding more resilient strategies. Overall, our results showcase the potential of reinforcement learning as a flexible decision-support tool for adaptive infrastructure planning under climate uncertainty.

</details>


### [176] [SAHOO: Safeguarded Alignment for High-Order Optimization Objectives in Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06333)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAHOO框架通过三个安全措施监控和控制递归自我改进中的对齐漂移：目标漂移指数检测器、约束保持检查和回归风险量化，在代码生成、数学推理和真实性任务中实现质量提升同时保持对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进从理论走向实践，但迭代自我修改存在微妙的对齐漂移风险，需要可部署的监控和控制框架来确保改进过程中的对齐保持。

Method: 提出SAHOO框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 目标漂移指数(GDI)，结合语义、词汇、结构和分布度量的多信号检测器；2) 约束保持检查，强制执行安全关键不变性；3) 回归风险量化，标记可能撤销先前增益的改进周期。

Result: 在189个任务（代码生成、数学推理、真实性）中，SAHOO实现显著质量提升：代码任务提升18.3%，推理任务提升16.8%，同时在两个领域保持约束，在真实性任务中保持低违规率。阈值在18个任务的验证集上校准。

Conclusion: SAHOO使递归自我改进过程中的对齐保持变得可测量、可部署且可系统验证，揭示了能力-对齐前沿的早期高效改进与后期对齐成本上升的权衡，以及领域特定张力（如流畅性与事实性）。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement is moving from theory to practice: modern systems can critique, revise, and evaluate their own outputs, yet iterative self-modification risks subtle alignment drift. We introduce SAHOO, a practical framework to monitor and control drift through three safeguards: (i) the Goal Drift Index (GDI), a learned multi-signal detector combining semantic, lexical, structural, and distributional measures; (ii) constraint preservation checks that enforce safety-critical invariants such as syntactic correctness and non-hallucination; and (iii) regression-risk quantification to flag improvement cycles that undo prior gains. Across 189 tasks in code generation, mathematical reasoning, and truthfulness, SAHOO produces substantial quality gains, including 18.3 percent improvement in code tasks and 16.8 percent in reasoning, while preserving constraints in two domains and maintaining low violations in truthfulness. Thresholds are calibrated on a small validation set of 18 tasks across three cycles. We further map the capability-alignment frontier, showing efficient early improvement cycles but rising alignment costs later and exposing domain-specific tensions such as fluency versus factuality. SAHOO therefore makes alignment preservation during recursive self-improvement measurable, deployable, and systematically validated at scale.

</details>


### [177] [Talk Freely, Execute Strictly: Schema-Gated Agentic AI for Flexible and Reproducible Scientific Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06394)
*Joel Strickland,Arjun Vijeta,Chris Moores,Oliwia Bodek,Bogdan Nenchev,Thomas Whitehead,Charles Phillips,Karl Tassenberg,Gareth Conduit,Ben Pellegrini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出schema-gated orchestration架构，通过模式验证作为执行边界，解决LLM科学工作流中确定性执行与对话灵活性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以将自然语言目标转换为可执行计算，但科学工作流需要确定性、可追溯性和治理保障，而LLM自主决策难以满足这些要求。研究发现工业R&D中存在确定性约束执行与无工作流刚性的对话灵活性之间的竞争需求。

Method: 提出schema-gated orchestration作为解决方案：模式成为组合工作流级别的强制执行边界，确保所有操作（包括跨步骤依赖）都通过机器可检查规范验证后才能执行。将需求操作化为执行确定性(ED)和对话灵活性(CF)，使用多模型协议评估20个系统，通过3个LLM家族的15个独立会话进行评分。

Result: 多模型LLM评分显示出高一致性（ED的Krippendorff α=0.80，CF的α=0.98），表明可作为人类专家评估的可重用替代方案。评估发现经验帕累托前沿，没有系统同时实现高灵活性和高确定性，但在生成式和以工作流为中心的两极之间出现了收敛区域。

Conclusion: schema-gated架构通过分离对话权限和执行权限，能够解耦灵活性与确定性的权衡。提炼出三个操作原则：执行前澄清、约束的计划-执行编排、工具到工作流级别的门控，以指导实际采用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can now translate a researcher's plain-language goal into executable computation, yet scientific workflows demand determinism, provenance, and governance that are difficult to guarantee when an LLM decides what runs. Semi-structured interviews with 18 experts across 10 industrial R&D stakeholders surface 2 competing requirements--deterministic, constrained execution and conversational flexibility without workflow rigidity--together with boundary properties (human-in-the-loop control and transparency) that any resolution must satisfy. We propose schema-gated orchestration as the resolving principle: the schema becomes a mandatory execution boundary at the composed-workflow level, so that nothing runs unless the complete action--including cross-step dependencies--validates against a machine-checkable specification.
  We operationalize the 2 requirements as execution determinism (ED) and conversational flexibility (CF), and use these axes to review 20 systems spanning 5 architectural groups along a validation-scope spectrum. Scores are assigned via a multi-model protocol--15 independent sessions across 3 LLM families--yielding substantial-to-near-perfect inter-model agreement (Krippendorff a=0.80 for ED and a=0.98 for CF), demonstrating that multi-model LLM scoring can serve as a reusable alternative to human expert panels for architectural assessment.
  The resulting landscape reveals an empirical Pareto front--no reviewed system achieves both high flexibility and high determinism--but a convergence zone emerges between the generative and workflow-centric extremes. We argue that a schema-gated architecture, separating conversational from execution authority, is positioned to decouple this trade-off, and distill 3 operational principles--clarification-before-execution, constrained plan-act orchestration, and tool-to-workflow-level gating--to guide adoption.

</details>


### [178] [Boosting deep Reinforcement Learning using pretraining with Logical Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06565)
*Zihan Ye,Phil Chau,Raban Emunds,Jannis Blüml,Cedric Derstroff,Quentin Delfosse,Oleg Arenz,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: H²RL：一种混合分层强化学习方法，通过逻辑选项预训练将符号结构注入神经策略，改善长期决策能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习智能体经常存在对齐问题，过度利用早期奖励信号。纯符号方法难以扩展到连续环境，需要结合神经方法的表达能力

Method: 提出混合分层强化学习框架，采用两阶段方法：1) 逻辑选项预训练引导策略远离短期奖励循环；2) 标准环境交互精炼最终策略，保持深度策略的表达能力

Result: 该方法在长期决策任务中表现一致优于强基准方法（神经、符号和神经符号方法），证明了混合方法的有效性

Conclusion: H²RL成功将符号结构注入神经强化学习智能体，解决了对齐问题，改善了长期目标导向行为，同时保持了深度策略的表达能力

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning agents are often misaligned, as they over-exploit early reward signals. Recently, several symbolic approaches have addressed these challenges by encoding sparse objectives along with aligned plans. However, purely symbolic architectures are complex to scale and difficult to apply to continuous settings. Hence, we propose a hybrid approach, inspired by humans' ability to acquire new skills. We use a two-stage framework that injects symbolic structure into neural-based reinforcement learning agents without sacrificing the expressivity of deep policies. Our method, called Hybrid Hierarchical RL (H^2RL), introduces a logical option-based pretraining strategy to steer the learning policy away from short-term reward loops and toward goal-directed behavior while allowing the final policy to be refined via standard environment interaction. Empirically, we show that this approach consistently improves long-horizon decision-making and yields agents that outperform strong neural, symbolic, and neuro-symbolic baselines.

</details>
